    For these four objectives, Peter has three basic multi-factor risk metrics: key-rate exposures, partial ’01s, and forward-bucket ’01s. For which of the four objectives is key-rate exposures best suited?
    \\彼得是一名金融分析师，他的老板要求他开发多因子风险模型，用于公司固定收益和衍生品投资组合的风险衡量。具体来说，有四个目标：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item I. 衡量和对冲债券组合风险，特别是可用流动性债券数量相对较少的情况，特别是近期发行的政府债券
        \item II. 衡量和对冲互换组合风险，特别是高流动性货币市场和互换工具数量较多的情况
        \item III. 衡量混合投资组合的风险，而不是其他证券，而是直接改变期限结构
        \item IV. 假设期限结构波动，但避免过多的“维度诅咒”；即，避免过多的相关性对
    \end{itemize}
    彼得有三个基本多因子风险指标：关键利率暴露，部分 '01s，和前向桶 '01s。对于哪一项目标，关键利率暴露最适合？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item I. only
    \\只有I.
    \item III. only
    \\只有III.
    \item I. and IV. only
    \\只有I.和IV.
    \item I., II., III. and IV.; i.e., all four
    \\I., II., III.和IV.; 即，所有四个
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关键利率暴露用于衡量和对冲债券组合风险，并且以少量最具流动性的债券为基础，通常是最新发行、接近面值的政府债券。
        \item 部分 '01s指标用于衡量和对冲掉期组合，或同时包含债券和掉期的投资组合风险，其依据是最具流动性的货币市场工具和掉期工具。由于这些工具的价格通常用于构建掉期曲线，因此在该方法下所用证券数量通常多于关键利率框架所用的数量。
        \item 最后，前向桶 '01s多用于掉期或同时包含债券与掉期的情境，其衡量的不是其他证券带来的风险，而是直接反映期限结构形状变化导致的风险。因此，前向桶 '01s 通常是理解组合曲线风险最直观的方法，但并不是最快找出需要中和该风险对冲方式的方法。本章最后还简要评论这些方法用于衡量组合波动率的应用。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{关键利率暴露、部分01与前向桶01的适用场景}{3}{2}

% --- Question 41 ---
\begin{question}
    The plot below illustrates an actual portfolio possibilities curve (PPC, dashed line). Two prominent portfolios (minimum variance and market portfolio) are also plotted.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·41·1.png} 
    \end{figure}
    The green point is the minimum variance portfolio, MVP, which has an expected return of $7.70\%$ and standard deviation, $\sigma$, of $10.0\%$. The red triangle is the Market Portfolio that has an expected return of $11.0\%$ and standard deviation, $\sigma$, of $13.7\%$. If the riskfree rate is $4.0\%$, what is the formula for the capital market line (CML)?

    下图显示了一个实际的投资组合可能性曲线（PPC，虚线）。图中还绘制了两个重要的投资组合（最小方差组合和市场投资组合）。
    \begin{figure}[!p]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·41·1.png} 
    \end{figure}
    绿色点表示最小方差投资组合（MVP），其期望收益为$7.70\%$，标准差$\sigma$为$10.0\%$。红色三角形表示市场投资组合，其期望收益为$11.0\%$，标准差$\sigma$为$13.7\%$。如果无风险利率为$4.0\%$，那么资本市场线（CML）的公式是什么？
    
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $E(R) = 0.040 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$
    \\资本市场线公式：$E(R) = 0.040 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$
    \item $E(R) = 0.137 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$
    \\资本市场线公式：$E(R) = 0.137 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$
    \item $E(R) = 0.040 + 0.375 \times \beta(P,M)$
    \\资本市场线公式：$E(R) = 0.040 + 0.375 \times \beta(P,M)$
    \item $E(R) = 0.077 + 0.511 \times \beta(P,M)$
    \\资本市场线公式：$E(R) = 0.077 + 0.511 \times \beta(P,M)$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item CML 的公式为 $E(R) = 0.040 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$。
        \item 资本市场线（CML）的纵截距必须等于无风险利率，此处为$4.0\%$。
        \item 其切线即为市场投资组合，CML的斜率为市场投资组合的夏普比率，计算方式为 $(11.0\% - 4.0\%)/13.7\%$。
        \item 因此，CML 的公式为 $E(R) = 0.040 + (0.110 - 0.040)/0.1370 \times \sigma(P) = 0.040 + 0.511 \times \sigma(P)$。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·41·2.png} 
    \end{figure}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{资本市场线（CML）的公式与市场投资组合的夏普比率}{3}{3}




% --- Question 42 ---
\begin{question}
    Jeremy simulates four time series: white noise, white noise with trend, a random walk, and a random walk with drift. They are plotted below.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·42.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Jeremy conducts an Augmented Dickey-Fuller (ADF) test with the R function \texttt{adf.test()}. According to GARP, the ADF is the most widely used unit root test. Its null hypothesis is that the series is a unit root process and its corresponding alternative hypothesis is that the series is stationary. Importantly, this \texttt{adf.test()} does allow an intercept and trend: it evaluates a trend-stationary process as stationary but evaluates a difference-stationary process (prior to transformation) as non-stationary.

    If Jeremy performs an ADF test on each of these four series, what is the most likely outcome?
    \\Jeremy 模拟了四个时间序列：白噪声，白噪声加趋势，随机游走，随机游走加漂移。它们绘制如下。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·42.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Jeremy 进行了一个增广迪基-富勒（ADF）测试，使用 R 函数 \texttt{adf.test()}。根据 GARP，ADF 是最广泛使用的单位根检验。其零假设是序列是一个单位根过程，其对应的备择假设是序列是平稳的。重要的是，这个 \texttt{adf.test()} 允许截距和趋势：它将趋势平稳过程评估为平稳，将差分平稳过程（在变换之前）评估为非平稳。
    如果 Jeremy 对这四个序列进行 ADF 测试，最可能的结果是什么？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item All four have low p-values; e.g., p-values below 0.050
    \\所有四个都有低p值；即，p值低于0.050
    \item All four have high p-values; e.g., p-values above 0.100
    \\所有四个都有高p值；即，p值高于0.100
    \item The random walks have low p-values but the white noise processes have high p-values
    \\随机游走有低p值，但白噪声过程有高p值
    \item The white noise processes have low p-values but the random walks have high p-values
    \\白噪声过程有低p值，但随机游走有高p值
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 白噪声过程有低p值，但随机游走有高p值。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{ADF 测试的假设与应用场景}{3}{3}



% --- Question 43 ---
\begin{question}
    The current level of a stock index is 7,790 while the risk-free rate is 4.0\% per annum with annual compounding. The dividend yield assumption is 1.50\% with continuous compounding. We can observe that the nine-month futures contract is currently trading at a price of 8,000. If an index arbitrage is possible, what is the index arbitrage trade?
    \\当前股票指数的水平为7,790，无风险利率为4.0\%每年，采用年复利。股息收益率假设为1.50\%，采用连续复利。我们可以观察到九个月远期合约目前交易价格为8,000。如果指数套利是可能的，那么指数套利交易是什么？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Buy the underlying stocks and short the futures contract
    \\买入底层股票并卖出远期合约
    \item Sell the underlying stocks and buy the futures contract
    \\卖出底层股票并买入远期合约
    \item Cannot figure because compound frequencies differ
    \\无法计算，因为复利频率不同
    \item The implied reverse cash-and-carry may not be possible if the ease lease rate on the borrowed stocks exceeds the risk-free rate
    \\如果借入股票的易租利率超过无风险利率，则隐含的反向现金和carry可能不可行
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 买入底层股票并卖出远期合约。
        \item 远期合约的理论价格为 $7,790 \times (1+4.0\%)^{0.75} \times \exp(-1.50\% \times 0.75) = 7,932.80$。
        \item 观察到的远期价格为8,000，交易价格过高，因此套利是现金和carry变种：买入底层股票并卖出远期合约。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{指数套利交易策略与远期合约价格计算}{3}{3}

% --- Question 44 ---



\begin{question}
    On May 28, 2019, Robin the Portfolio Manager considers the purchase of \dollar100.0 million face amount of the U.S. Treasury 2\,3/8\%s (2.375\%) due November 15, 2028, at a cost of \dollar90,250,000. She refers to this as her ``bullet'' investment. After an analysis of the interest rate environment, she is comfortable with the pricing of the bond at a yield of 3.60\% and with its duration of 8.32 years.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·44.png} 
    \end{figure}
    However, upon further inspection, Robin considers an alternative to the above-mentioned bullet investment in the 10-year 2\,3/8\%s. Specifically, the alternative is a ``barbell'' investment in the shorter maturity 5-year 2\,1/2\%s and the longer maturity 30-year 4\,5/8\%s. The barbell would be constructed to have the same COST and DURATION as the bullet investment. Which of the following is nearest to the convexity of the alternative barbell portfolio?
    \\
    在2019年5月28日，Robin the Portfolio Manager 考虑购买面值为\dollar100.0百万的美国财政部2\,3/8\%s（2.375\%）债券，到期日为2028年11月15日，成本为\dollar90,250,000。她将其称为她的“子弹”投资。在利率环境分析后，她对债券的定价为3.60\%，期限为8.32年。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·44.png} 
    \end{figure}
    然而，进一步检查后，Robin 考虑了一种替代上述子弹投资的10年期2\,3/8\%s。具体来说，替代是一种“哑铃”投资，在较短到期5年期2\,1/2\%s和较长到期30年期4\,5/8\%s。哑铃将构造为具有与子弹投资相同的成本和期限。下列哪一项最接近替代哑铃投资组合的凸性？
\end{question}



\begin{choices}
    \item 50.4 years${}^2$
    \\哑铃投资组合的凸性最接近50.4年$^2$
    \item 78.4 years${}^2$
    \\哑铃投资组合的凸性最接近78.4年$^2$
    \item 136.7 years${}^2$
    \\哑铃投资组合的凸性最接近136.7年$^2$
    \item 305.9 years${}^2$
    \\哑铃投资组合的凸性最接近305.9年$^2$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的答案是：136.7年$^2$
        \item 我们需要同时求解两个未知数：
        $$V(5) + V(30) = 90.250, \text{ 和}$$
        $$\frac{V(5)}{90.250} \times 4.55 + \frac{V(30)}{90.250} \times 15.96 = 8.32.$$
        \item 解得 $V(5) = \dollar60.43$ 和 $V(30) = \dollar29.82$，因此哑铃的期限为：
        $$D(\text{barbell}) = \frac{\dollar60.43}{90.250} \times 4.55 + \frac{\dollar29.82}{90.250} \times 15.96 = 8.32 \text{ years}$$
        \item (根据定义！)；哑铃的凸性为：
        $$C(\text{barbell}) = \frac{\dollar60.43}{90.250} \times 23.6 + \frac{\dollar29.82}{90.250} \times 365.9 = 136.70 \text{ years}^2$$
    \end{itemize}
    
\end{explanation}

\checklist{子弹型与哑铃型组合：久期匹配与凸性}{3}{2}

% --- Question 45 ---
\begin{question}
    Securitization is a trend that is over fifty years old: The Housing and Urban Development Act of 1968 gave birth to Ginnie Mae (https://www.ginniemae.gov/) in an effort to promote homeownership by way of guaranteeing residential mortgage-backed securities (MBS). As GARP explains (see Figure 4.2 of Chapter 4) there have been several milestones along the way. Each of the following statements about securitization is true EXCEPT which is false?
    \\Securitization 是一个已经存在了五十年的趋势：1968年的住房和城市发展法案催生了 Ginnie Mae（https://www.ginniemae.gov/），旨在通过保证住宅抵押贷款支持证券（MBS）来促进住房所有权。根据 GARP 的解释（见第4章的图4.2），一路上有几个里程碑。下列关于证券化的陈述中哪一项是正确的，除了哪一项是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Securitization offers at least some diversification of credit risk
    \\证券化提供了至少一些信用风险的分散
    \item Securitization transfers a substantial amount of risk, including but not limited to credit risk, from the originator's balance sheet to investors
    \\证券化将大量的风险，包括但不限于信用风险，从发起人的资产负债表转移到投资者
    \item The 2007-2009 global financial crisis (GFC) discredited the principles of securitization as evidenced by the subsequent evaporation of asset-backed securities (ABS)
    \\2007-2009全球金融危机（GFC）动摇了证券化的原则，正如随后资产支持证券（ABS）的蒸发所表明的那样
    \item Securitization enables originate-to-distribute (OTD) but the 2007-2009 crisis witnessed poor securitization risk management that allowed too much systemic concentration of risk
    \\证券化允许发起-分销（OTD），但2007-2009危机见证了糟糕的证券化风险管理，允许过多的系统性风险集中
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 金融危机既没有使证券化失去信誉，也没有看到资产支持证券消失。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 前两个正确的陈述（即A和B）体现了经典证券化的两个基本特征：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item 通过将信用敏感资产进行池化，实现了分散化；
            \item 通过向投资者发行负债（票据），将信用风险转移给投资者。
        \end{itemize}
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{证券化交易的信用风险分散与转移特征}{3}{3}

% --- Question 46 ---
\begin{question}
    Consider the following seasonal time series model that is represented with lag notation:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·46.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Note that $\phi(1) = 0.530$ and $\phi(s) = 0.260$. In regard to this seasonal model, each of the following statements is true EXCEPT which is false?
    \\考虑以下季节性时间序列模型，用滞后表示法表示：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·46.png} 
    \end{figure}
    注意$\phi(1) = 0.530$ 和 $\phi(s) = 0.260$。关于这个季节性模型，下列陈述中哪一项是正确的，除了哪一项是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item This seasonal model is a restricted AR(13) model
    \\这个季节性模型是一个受限的AR(13)模型
    \item The coefficient on lag 13 is -0.13780
    \\滞后13的系数为-0.13780
    \item The coefficients on lags 2 through 11 are zero
    \\滞后2到11的系数为零
    \item This seasonal model is deterministic because lag 12 is a dummy variable
    \\这个季节性模型是确定的，因为滞后12是一个虚拟变量
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 虚拟变量是分类的0或1，但这是一个随机（且平稳）的ARMA模型，其系数$0.26$对滞后$12$项加权；因此，这是ARMA模型的一个特例，可以用符号表示为ARMA$(1,0) \times (1,0)_{12}$。
    \end{itemize}

    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于(A)、(B)和(C)，每个都是正确的。
        \item 具体而言：这个季节性模型是一个受限的AR$(13)$模型。
        \item 滞后$13$的系数为$-0.13780$，因为：
        $$[1 - \phi(1)L] \times [1 - \phi(s)L^{12}] \times Y(t) = \delta + \varepsilon(t) \rightarrow [1 - \phi(1)L - \phi(s)L^{12} + \phi(1) \times \phi(s)L^{13}] \times Y(t) = \delta + \varepsilon(t) \rightarrow Y(t) = \delta + \phi(1) \times Y(t-1) + \phi(s) \times Y(t-12) - \phi(1) \times \phi(s) \times Y(t-13) + \varepsilon(t)$$
        \item 滞后$2$到$11$的系数为零。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{季节性时间序列模型的特征与形式化表示}{3}{3}

% --- Question 47 ---
\begin{question}
    The current spot exchange rate between the euro (EUR) and quote currency YYY is 1.3000 EURYYY. The EUR risk-free rate is $4.0\%$ and the YYY risk-free rate is $2.0\%$; each interest rate is per annum with annual compounding. According to covered interest rate parity (CIRP), which is nearest to the forward rate in nine months ($T = 0.75$ years) quoted in points?
    \\当前欧元（EUR）和报价货币YYY的即期汇率是1.3000 EURYYY。欧元无风险利率为4.0\%，YYY无风险利率为2.0\%；每个利率均为年化，采用年复利。根据覆盖利率平价（CIRP），九个月（$T = 0.75$年）的远期汇率最接近多少点？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item -12,576
    \item -188.0
    \item +33.0
    \item +291.0
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item -188.0 远期汇率点
        \item 根据CIRP，$1.30 \text{ EURYYY} \times [(1.02/1.04)]^{(9/12)} = 1.28120$，$(1.28120 - 1.3000) \times 10,000 = -188.0$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{远期汇率点计算与利率平价关系}{3}{3}

% --- Question 48 ---
\begin{question}
    A speculative (aka, junk) bond has 15.0 years to maturity and pays a semi-annual $6\ 1/8$ coupon; i.e., its coupon rate is $6.125\%$ payable semi-annually. Its yield is $11.00\%$. If we assume a reasonable shock value (i.e., less than 100 basis points), which of the following is \textbf{nearest} to the bond's effective convexity?
    \\一个投机性（即，垃圾）债券有15年到期，每年支付两次$6\ 1/8$的利息；即，其票面利率为$6.125\%$，每年支付两次。其收益率为$11.00\%$。如果我们假设一个合理的冲击值（即，小于100个基点），下列哪一项最接近债券的有效凸性？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $12.4 \text{ years}^2$
    \\债券的有效凸性最接近12.4年$^2$
    \item $98.0 \text{ years}^2$
    \\债券的有效凸性最接近98.0年$^2$
    \item $124.3 \text{ years}^2$
    \\债券的有效凸性最接近124.3年$^2$
    \item Cannot answer because face value is not given
    \\无法回答，因为面值未给出
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $98.0 \text{年}^2 = (1/\dollar64.574) \times [(\dollar61.996 + \dollar67.310 - 2\times\dollar64.574)/0.0050^2]$，这里选择50个基点（bps）作为冲击值。
        \item 在本题中，初始价格 $= -\text{PV}(0.1100/2, 15*2, 100*0.06125/2, 100) = \dollar64.574$，如果收益率的冲击为$\pm 50$个基点，则：
        \item $\text{价格}(\text{收益率} + 0.0050) = -\text{PV}(0.1150/2, 15*2, 100*0.06125/2, 100) = \text{\dollar}61.996$；
        \item $\text{价格}(\text{收益率} - 0.0050) = -\text{PV}(0.1050/2, 15*2, 100*0.06125/2, 100) = \text{\dollar}67.310$。
        \item 针对错误选项(D)，需注意如果我们（始终一致地）将票面金额（面值）改为任何数值，凸性不会发生变化。
        \item 此外，虽然当我们改变冲击值时凸性会有极小的变化，但在冲击小于$1.0\%$的情况下，凸性大约接近98.0年$^2$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{债券凸性计算与冲击值选择}{3}{3}

% --- Question 49 ---
\begin{question}
    In regard to limits policies, GARP explains that ``optimal risk governance requires the ability to link risk appetite and limits to specific business practices. Accordingly, appropriate limits need to be developed for each business as well as for the specific risks associated with the business (as well as for the entire portfolio of the enterprise).'' Most institutions set two types of limits, tier 1 (one) and tier 2 (two) limits. About these limits, which of the following is TRUE?
    \\关于限制政策，GARP 解释说“最佳风险治理需要将风险偏好与特定业务实践联系起来。因此，需要为每个业务以及与业务相关的特定风险（还包括整个企业的投资组合）制定适当的限制。”大多数机构设置两种类型的限制，一级（一级）和二级（二级）限制。关于这些限制，哪一项是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Firms should choose and either adopt tier 1 or tier 2 limits but not both simultaneously
    \\公司应该选择并采用一级或二级限制，但不能同时采用
    \item Tier 1 (tier one) limit exceedances must be cleared or corrected immediately, while tier 2 (tier two) exceedances are less urgent and can be cleared within a few days or a week.
    \\一级（一级）限制超过必须立即清除或纠正，而二级（二级）限制超过不那么紧急，可以在几天或一周内清除。
    \item Tier 2 (tier two) limits are specific and often include an overall limit by asset class, an overall stress-test limit, and a maximum drawdown limit
    \\二级（二级）限制是特定的，通常包括资产类别的总体限制、总体压力测试限制和最大回撤限制
    \item Tier 1 (tier one) limits are more generalized and relate to areas of business activity as well as aggregated exposures categorized by credit rating, industry, maturity, and region
    \\一级（一级）限制更通用，与业务活动区域以及按信用评级、行业、期限和地区分类的聚合暴露有关
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 一级（一级）限制超过必须立即清除或纠正，而二级（二级）限制超过不那么紧急，可以在几天或一周内清除。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \begin{itemize}
        \item GARP解释说，“大多数机构设置两种类型的限制。1. 一级限制是特定的，通常包括资产类别的总体限制、总体压力测试限制和最大回撤限制。2. 二级限制更通用，与业务活动区域以及按信用评级、行业、期限和地区分类的聚合暴露有关，等等。”[1]
        \item 针对(A)、(C)和(D)，每个都是错误的。
    \end{itemize}
   [1] 2020 FRM 一级：风险管理基础，第10版。培生学习解决方案，2019年10月
\end{explanation}

\checklist{风险管理基础原则与限制政策类型}{3}{3}

% --- Question 50 ---
\begin{question}
    Patrick generated a simple regression line for a sample of 50 pairwise observations. After generating the regression model, he ran R’s built-in plot(model) function which produces a standard set of regression diagnostics. These four plots are displayed below.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·50.png} 
    \end{figure}
    About these diagnostic plots, which of the following statements is TRUE?
    \\Patrick 生成了一组50个成对观测值的简单回归线。在生成回归模型后，他运行 R 的内置 plot(model) 函数，生成一组标准的回归诊断图。这些四个图显示如下。关于这些诊断图，哪一项是正确的？
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·50.png} 
    \end{figure}
    关于这些诊断图，哪一项是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item There are many outliers
    \\数据中有很多异常值
    \item The data is significantly heteroskedastic
    \\数据具有显著的异方差性
    \item The residuals are a bit heavy-tailed (non-normal) on the right side
    \\残差在右侧略微重尾（非正态）
    \item The residuals reveal that the relationship between the explanatory and response variable is non-linear
    \\残差揭示解释变量和响应变量之间的关系是非线性的
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{选项C是正确的。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 残差在右侧略微重尾（非正态）。
    \end{itemize}

    \begin{itemize}
        \item 虽然在 Residual vs Fitted 图中可以看到一些潜在的异常值，但 Residuals vs. Leverage 图显示没有观测值的库克距离大于0.5（即没有点高于红色虚线）。没有异方差性的迹象，所有图表总体支持近似的线性关系。具体来说，各个图表如下解释：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item \textit{Residuals vs Fitted}（残差与拟合值图）：该图展示了残差与拟合值的关系。我们希望残差在零附近随机分布（该图确实如此），点分布较为均匀，说明不存在明显的异方差性；也就是说，没有表现出异方差的图案，异常值不多。这是一个表现良好的残差图，表明回归模型线性效果不错。
            \item \textit{Normal Q-Q}（正态Q-Q图）：如果残差分布是正态的，点会大致在直线附近。但可以看到该图在右侧有明显的重尾现象。
            \item \textit{Scale-location}（尺度-位置图）：与 Residuals vs Fitted 图类似，但残差已标准化。也用于评估异方差性。但这里同样没有发现明显的非恒定方差的证据。
            \item \textit{Residuals vs Leverage}（残差与杠杆值图）：红色虚线代表库克距离0.5，没有观测点在此线外（例如左上角没有点），因此没有明显的异常值案例。
        \end{itemize}
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{机器学习线性回归模型的诊断图表分析}{3}{3}

% --- Question 51 ---
\begin{question}
    On January 15th of Year 1, a company decides to hedge the planned purchase of 100,000 bushels of corn thirteen months later (on February 15 of Year 2). Below are displayed the per-bushel futures prices of three selected contracts on four different dates.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·51.png}
    \end{figure}
   
    Assume the following: at the time of ultimate purchase (February 15 of Year 2), the spot price is $\dollar6.47$ per bushel when the final contract’s basis is $\dollar6.47 - \dollar6.50 = -\dollar0.03$. If the company employs a stack-and-roll strategy, what is the company’s net (i.e., after hedging) cost to acquire the corn? [inspired by GARP’s EOC Question 8.20]
    \\在1月15日，一家公司决定对13个月后（即2月15日）计划购买的100,000蒲式耳玉米进行对冲。下面显示了三个选定合约在四个不同日期的每蒲式耳期货价格。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·51.png}
    \end{figure}
    假设：在最终购买时（2月15日），现货价格为每蒲式耳$\dollar6.47$，最终合约的基差为$\dollar6.47 - \dollar6.50 = -\dollar0.03$。如果公司采用堆叠和滚动策略，公司购买玉米的净成本（即对冲后）是多少？[inspired by GARP’s EOC Question 8.20]
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $\dollar45,000$
    \item $\dollar602,000$
    \item $\dollar647,000$
    \item $\dollar650,000$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 在本题情境中，公司未来需要购买玉米，因此对冲方式为做多期货的“堆叠和滚动”策略（stack-and-roll）：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 2021年5月合约在1月15日以$\dollar5.85$/蒲式耳买入，2021年4月以$\dollar6.00$卖出；
        \item 2021年9月合约在4月以$\dollar6.10$买入，8月以$\dollar6.30$卖出；
        \item 2022年3月合约在8月以$\dollar6.40$买入，2022年2月以$\dollar6.50$卖出。
    \end{itemize}
        \item 每份合约的总收益为 $5,000\times[(6.00-5.85)+(6.30-6.10)+(6.50-6.40)] = 5,000\times 0.45 = \$2,250.00$。公司总计需要 $100,000 \div 5,000 = 20$ 份合约，因此总对冲收益为 $20\times\$2,250.00 = \$45,000$，即每蒲式耳收益 \$0.45，共 100,000 蒲式耳，等于 \$45,000.00。所以，玉米的对冲后净购买成本为
        \[
            (6.47 \times 100,000) - 45,000 = \$602,000.00
        \]
    \end{itemize}

\end{explanation}

\checklist{金融市场的产品与对冲策略}{3}{3}

% --- Question 52 ---
\begin{question}
Consider a 9-year bond with a semi-annual $10.0\%$ coupon that has a current price of $\dollar119.780$ and a yield of $7.000\%$. If the yield drops to $6.850\%$, the bond's price increases to $\dollar120.900$. If this is the case, then which of the following is \textbf{nearest} to the bond's dollar value of '01 (DV01)?
\\考虑一个9年期的债券，每年支付两次$10.0\%$的利息，当前价格为$\dollar119.780$，收益率为$7.000\%$。如果收益率下降到$6.850\%$，债券的价格增加到$\dollar120.900$。如果是这种情况，那么哪一项最接近债券的'01美元价值（DV01）？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item -0.0121
    \item 0.0121
    \item 0.0747
    \item 1.1200
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
\textbf{C选项正确。} 
\begin{itemize}
\item 当收益率下降15个基点时，债券价格的变化为 $\dollar120.900 - \dollar119.780 = \dollar1.120$，所以每个基点的价格变化为 $\dollar1.120/15 = 0.0746667$。需要注意的是，严格来说（技术上），DV01 指的是收益率下降1个基点对应的价格变化。收益率上升1个基点时的价格变化几乎相同，但不会完全一致。
\end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{固定收益风险衡量指标的计算}{3}{3}

% --- Question 53 ---
\begin{question}
    GARP explains that “risk management must be implemented across the entire enterprise under a set of unified policies and methodologies. (This is called enterprise risk management ...). The infrastructure of risk management, which includes both physical resources and clearly defined operational processes, must be up to the task of an enterprise-wide scope.”[1] However, it is a difficult and daunting task to evaluate the fitness of a firm or bank’s risk management system. Among the following features, indicators, or characteristics, which is MOST LIKELY to signify that the firm is serious about its risk management process?

    [1] 2020 FRM Part I: Foundations of Risk Management, 10th Edition. Pearson Learning Solutions, 10/2019.

    GARP 解释道：“风险管理必须在整个企业范围内，根据一套统一的政策和方法来实施（这被称为企业风险管理……）。风险管理的基础设施，包括物理资源和明确定义的操作流程，必须能够满足企业范围管理的需求。”[1] 然而，评估一家公司或银行风险管理体系的适用性是一项困难且艰巨的任务。在下列特征、指标或特性中，哪一项最有可能表明该公司对其风险管理流程非常重视？

    [1] 2020年FRM一级考试《风险管理基础》第10版，培生学习解决方案，2019年10月。
    
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item The firm's risk manager(s) is a well-compensated member of the executive staff with compelling career opportunities
    \\公司风险经理（们）是享有丰厚薪酬的执行人员，拥有诱人的职业发展机会
    \item At least one-third of executive compensation plans consist of stock options, or in the case of private firms, phantom stock options
    \\至少三分之一的执行薪酬计划包括股票期权，或者在私人公司的情况下，包括虚幻的股票期权
    \item The board has quantified the firm's agency costs and can show that agency costs are within a quantifiable confidence interval, or at least below an upper numeric bound
    \\董事会已经量化了公司的代理成本，并可以证明代理成本在可量化的置信区间内，或者至少低于一个上界数字
    \item The board's audit committee reports to the board's risk management committee and every member of the board is also a member of the board's risk management committee
    \\董事会审计委员会向董事会风险管理委员会报告，并且董事会每个成员也是董事会风险管理委员会的成员
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item GARP 认为，真正考验公司对风险经理看法的是如何对待人力资本。
        \item GARP 解释说：“衡量风险管理过程严肃性的一个方法是检查雇用的员工以及风险经理在公司层级中的地位：风险经理是否被视为执行人员，并且这个职位是否能带来其他职业机会？风险经理有多独立？他或她拥有什么权力？他或她向谁报告？风险经理相对于其他因绩效（例如交易员）而获得奖励的员工来说，是否得到了很好的补偿？企业伦理文化有多强大和有弹性，能够抵御不良行为者的行动？公司是否制定了明确的伦理标准，并且这些标准是否被积极执行？”
    \end{itemize}
    
    [1] 2020 FRM 一级：风险管理基础，第10版。培生学习解决方案，2019年10月。
\end{explanation}

\checklist{企业风险管理（ERM）与风险管理有效性指标}{3}{3}

% --- Question 54 ---
\begin{question}
    Mary works for an insurance company and she has regressed medical costs (aka, the response or dependent variable) for a sample of patients against four independent variables: AGE, BMI, SMOKER, and CHARITY. The sample’s average age is 38.51 years. Body mass index (BMI) is mass divided by height squared and the sample’s average BMI is 22.16. SMOKER is a dummy variable where zero indicates a non-smoker and 1 indicates a smoker; the sample’s average SMOKER value is 0.163 which indicates that $16.3\%$ of the sample are smokers. CHARITY is the dollar amount of charitable spending in the last year; the sample average is $\dollar490.70$ donated to charity in the last year. Mary’s regression results are displayed below.

    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·54.png} 
    \end{figure}

    Each of the following statements is true about these regression results EXCEPT which is false?
    \\Mary 对一个样本的病人进行了医疗成本（aka，响应或因变量）的回归，使用四个自变量：AGE，BMI，SMOKER，和 CHARITY。样本的平均年龄是38.51岁。身体质量指数（BMI）是体重除以身高平方，样本的平均BMI是22.16。SMOKER 是一个虚拟变量，其中零表示非吸烟者，1表示吸烟者；样本的平均SMOKER值是0.163，这意味着样本中有16.3\%的人是吸烟者。CHARITY 是去年慈善支出的金额；样本的平均值是$\dollar490.70$捐赠给慈善机构。Mary 的回归结果如下。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·54.png} 
    \end{figure}
    关于这些回归结果，哪一项是正确的，除了哪一项是错误的？
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item The sample size is 43 patients
        \\样本大小是43个病人
        \item Mary can reject a null hypothesis that all explanatory variables (jointly) have zero coefficients
        \\Mary 可以拒绝所有解释变量（ jointly）具有零系数的零假设
        \item Mary can infer that patient medical cost is positively associated with each of AGE, BMI, and, on average, is greater for a smoker
        \\Mary 可以推断出病人医疗成本与AGE，BMI和平均来说，吸烟者的医疗成本更高
        \item Mary should suspect problematic multicollinearity because the intercept is suspiciously negative and the adjusted R-squared is too near to the unadjusted R-squared
        \\Mary 应该怀疑多重共线性，因为截距是可疑的负值，并且调整后的R平方与未调整的R平方太接近
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    D
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 错误的（三重错误！）。多重共线性的典型症状是高$R^2$但只有少数显著的t统计量（但所有t统计量都是显著的）。负截距本身并不可疑：它只是样本外的。最后，给定$n = 43$和$k = 4$，根据调整后的$R^2 = 1 - [(1-R^2)*(n-1)/(n-k-1)]$，调整后的$R^2$在定义上接近未调整的$R^2$。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 针对(A)、(B)和(C)，每个都是正确的
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 样本大小是43个病人
        \item Mary 可以拒绝所有解释变量（ jointly）具有零系数的零假设
        \item Mary 可以推断出病人医疗成本与AGE，BMI和平均来说，吸烟者的医疗成本更高
    \end{itemize}
    \end{itemize}
    \end{explanation}
    
\checklist{多元回归结果的解释与多重共线性判断}{3}{2}

% --- Question 55 ---
\begin{question}
    Today an exchange introduced a new futures contract. The open interest started at zero. Because it is a new contract, there were only the following four trades:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Trader Adam (TA) buys (aka, takes a long position in) 1,000 contracts from Trader Betty (TB) who is the seller (aka, takes a short position) and both are new positions
        \item Trader Charles (TC) buys 300 contracts, in a new position, from Trader Adam (TA) who closes out 300 of his contracts
        \item Trader Denise (TD) sells 225 contracts, in a new position, to Trader Betty (TB), the buyer who closes out 225 of her contracts
        \item Trader Betty (TB) delivers on 175 of her short contracts to Trader Adam (TA) who takes delivery on 175 of his long contracts
    \end{itemize}
    What is the open interest, after the above four trades, at the end of the day?
    \\今天一个交易所推出了一种新的期货合约。初始的未平仓合约数量为零。因为这是一个新的合约，只有以下四笔交易：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Trader Adam (TA) 从 Trader Betty (TB) 手中买入（aka，建立多头头寸）1,000 份合约，Trader Betty (TB) 是卖家（aka，建立空头头寸），双方都是新的头寸
        \item Trader Charles (TC) 买入 300 份合约，建立新的多头头寸，从 Trader Adam (TA) 手中买入，Trader Adam (TA) 平仓 300 份合约
        \item Trader Denise (TD) 卖出 225 份合约，建立新的空头头寸，卖给 Trader Betty (TB)，Trader Betty (TB) 平仓 225 份合约
        \item Trader Betty (TB) 向 Trader Adam (TA) 交付 175 份空头合约，Trader Adam (TA) 接收 175 份多头合约
    \end{itemize}
    在上述四笔交易后，当天的未平仓合约数量是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Zero
    \\零
    \item 300 contracts
    \\300 份合约
    \item 825 contracts
    \\825 份合约
    \item 1,000 contracts
    \\1,000 份合约
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：825 份合约
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{期货未平仓合约与交割}{3}{2}

% --- Question 56 ---
\begin{question}
    Assume that the following upward-sloping zero rate (aka, spot rate) curve prevails:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·56.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Tuckman introduces the concept of a coupon effect in Chapter 3. Each of the following statements relates to this coupon effect and is necessarily true EXCEPT which statement is false?

    假设目前市场上存在如下上升趋势的零利率（即即期利率）曲线：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·56.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Tuckman 在第三章介绍了债券的息票效应（coupon effect）概念。下列每个陈述都与这种息票效应相关，除了哪一项是错误的以外，其余均正确？请选出唯一不正确的说法。
    
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Given this spot rate curve, the two-year par rate (aka, par yield) must be less than $3.4\%$
    \\给定这个即期利率曲线，两年期票面利率（aka，票面收益率）必须小于$3.4\%$
    \item Given this spot rate curve, a zero-coupon bond with a two-year maturity must have a yield (yield to maturity) of $3.4\%$
    \\给定这个即期利率曲线，两年期零息债券必须有一个收益率（到期收益率）为$3.4\%$
    \item Given this spot rate curve, a coupon-bearing bond with a two-year maturity must have a yield (yield to maturity) that is less than $3.4\%$
    \\给定这个即期利率曲线，两年期附息债券必须有一个收益率（到期收益率）小于$3.4\%$
    \item Given this spot rate curve, the yields (yields to maturity) of all bonds with two-year maturities must be identical due to the Law of One Price
    \\给定这个即期利率曲线，所有两年期债券的收益率（到期收益率）必须相同，因为根据一价定律
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 一价定律有效地要求每个期限只有一个折现因子，相当于说每个期限只能有一个零利率。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 选项D之所以是错误的，正是因为“息票效应”：两年期债券的收益率会随着其票息的不同而变化。债券的到期收益率是所有相关即期利率的复杂平均数，并且重要的是，它取决于债券的现金流结构。
    \end{itemize}

    \begin{itemize}
        \item 至于(A)、(B)和(C)，每一个都是正确的。具体来说，对于(C)来说，由该零利率曲线隐含的两年期平价收益率（par yield）为$3.3621\%$，因为当两年期债券的票息为$3.3621\%$时，其理论价格正好等于$\dollar100.00$，因此它的到期收益率也为$3.3621\%$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{即期利率曲线、票面收益率与息票效应}{3}{2}

% --- Question 57 ---
\begin{question}
    To hedge risk, the firm’s toolbox includes derivatives such as swaps, futures, forwards, and options. About the use of derivatives to hedge, each of the following is true EXCEPT which is inaccurate?

    为了对冲风险，公司的工具箱包括衍生品，如互换、期货、远期和期权。关于使用衍生品对冲，下列每个陈述都是正确的，除了哪一项是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Derivatives represent a decision to transfer (or mitigate) the risk(s) when the firm neither wants to retain nor avoid the risk
    \\衍生品代表了一种决策，当公司既不想保留风险，也不想避免风险时，转移（或减轻）风险
    \item Because hedging can only mute the volatility of accrual-based earnings, hedging cannot increase (or even alter) the firm's cash flows and therefore cannot influence agency risks
    \\因为对冲只能减弱基于应计的收入波动，对冲不能增加（甚至改变）公司的现金流，因此不能影响代理风险
    \item A large conglomerate (i.e., a firm with multiple business units operating in different industries/sectors) is more likely to create natural hedges than a small, focused firm
    \\一个大型多元化企业（即，一个在不同行业/部门运营多个业务单位的企业）更有可能创造自然对冲，而不是一个小型、专注的企业
    \item Airlines can cross-hedge the cost of jet fuel with futures contracts on (crude or heating) oil, but if the commodity's price drops rapidly, unhedged airlines are likely to outperform their hedged competitors
    \\航空公司可以使用期货合约对冲航空燃油成本，但如果商品价格迅速下跌，未对冲的航空公司可能比对冲的竞争对手表现更好
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 对冲可以改变现金流，也可以利用代理风险
        \item GARP 解释说，“[对冲] 对于投资者来说是有意义的，如果它被用作增加公司现金流（而不是减少股权投资者风险）的工具。
        \item 例如，公司可能需要在接下来三年内为客户提供稳定的价格，这可能在没有对冲关键成本投入的情况下是不可能的。如果像这样对冲增加了客户需求，那么股权投资者就会高兴... 
        \item 如果公司使用对冲来减少其税负（例如，通过稳定一年到下一年的收入），那么股权投资者也会高兴。再次强调，对冲会增加税后收入。
        \item 最后，股权投资者不是唯一的利益相关者，当然也不是唯一的决策者。管理者、监管者和一般员工期望公司财务稳健，并受到突发事件的保护。不太合法地，管理者可能使用对冲来确保他们的公司满足影响其声誉和薪酬的关键短期目标（例如，股票分析师期望）。风险经理需要密切关注衍生品如何利用代理风险。”[1]
    \end{itemize}
    
    [1] 2020 FRM 一级：风险管理基础，第10版。培生学习解决方案，2019年10月。
\end{explanation}

\checklist{衍生品对冲与现金流、代理风险}{3}{2}

% --- Question 58 ---
\begin{question}
    Debra is an economist who is interested in the relationship between consumer spending and the gross domestic product (GDP). From the FRED database at the Fed's Bank of St. Louis (https://fred.stlouisfed.org/) she collects quarterly data from 1980 through the first quarter of 2020; her series includes $n = 161$ quarters of data. She regresses consumer spending (C\_SPEND), as the response (aka, dependent) variable against GDP as the explanatory (aka, independent) variable. Each series is not a level, but rather a seasonally adjusted percent change. The regression results are displayed below.

    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·58.png} 
    \end{figure}

    Additionally, Debra calculates the (univariate) standard deviation of each variable over the period: $\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND}) = 2.484$ and $\sigma(\text{GDP}) = 3.412$. In regard to these regression results, each of the following statements is true EXCEPT which is false?
    \\Debra 是一位经济学家，她对消费者支出和国内生产总值（GDP）之间的关系感兴趣。她从联邦储备银行圣路易斯（https://fred.stlouisfed.org/）的 FRED 数据库收集了1980年至2020年第一季度的季度数据；她的系列包括161个季度的数据。她将消费者支出（C\_SPEND）作为响应变量（aka，因变量），将GDP作为解释变量（aka，自变量）进行回归。每个系列都不是水平，而是季节调整后的百分比变化。回归结果如下。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·58.png} 
    \end{figure}
    此外，Debra 计算了每个变量在时期内的（单变量）标准差：$\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND}) = 2.484$ 和 $\sigma(\text{GDP}) = 3.412$。关于这些回归结果，下列每个陈述都是正确的，除了哪一项是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item The coefficients are jointly insignificant because each two-sided $95.0\%$ confidence interval contain zero
    \\系数联合不显著，因为每个双侧95.0\%的置信区间都包含零
    \item The GDP's t-statistic ("t-stat") of 7.3285 is equal to the coefficient estimate (0.3659) divided by its standard error (0.0499)
    \\GDP的t统计量（"t-stat"）为7.3285，等于系数估计值（0.3659）除以其标准误差（0.0499）
    \item Given the variables' cross-volatility, $\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND})/\sigma(\text{GDP}) = 2.484 \div 3.412 = 0.7280$, the correlation between the variables is approximately 0.50
    \\给定变量之间的交叉波动性，$\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND})/\sigma(\text{GDP}) = 2.484 \div 3.412 = 0.7280$，变量之间的相关性约为0.50
    \item The coefficient of determination (R-squared; aka, $R^2$) is about 0.25 such that we can say about $25\%$ in the variation in consumer spending (C\_SPEND) is explained by gross domestic product (GPD)
    \\判定系数（R-squared；aka，$R^2$）约为0.25，因此我们可以认为消费者支出（C\_SPEND）变异的$25\%$可以由国内生产总值（GPD）解释
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 错误的：每个（aka，两个）系数都是显著的（as we can immediately observe by their small p-values），因此没有一个双侧95.0\%的置信区间包含零。
        \item  $95.0\%$置信水平下，截距系数的置信区间为$0.9000 \pm 1.96 \times 0.3156 = (0.281, 1.518)$。
        \item $95.0\%$置信水平下，斜率系数的置信区间为$0.3659 \pm 1.96 \times 0.0499 = (0.268, 0.464)$。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item GDP的t统计量（"t-stat"）为7.3285，等于系数估计值（0.3659）除以其标准误差（0.0499）
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 给定变量之间的交叉波动性，$\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND})/\sigma(\text{GDP}) = 2.484 \div 3.412 = 0.7280$，变量之间的相关性约为0.50。因为$\beta(\text{C\_SPEND, GDP}) = \rho(\text{C\_SPEND, GPD}) \times \sigma(\text{C\_SPEND})/\sigma(\text{GDP})$，所以$\rho(\text{C\_SPEND, GPD}) = \beta(\text{C\_SPEND, GDP}) \times \sigma(\text{GDP})/\sigma(\text{C\_SPEND})$；在这种情况下，$\rho(\text{C\_SPEND, GPD}) = 0.3659 \times 3.412/2.484 = 0.5026$或约为0.50。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 判定系数（$R^2$）约为0.25，因此我们可以认为消费者支出（C\_SPEND）变异的$25\%$可以由国内生产总值（GPD）解释。在单变量回归中，$R^2$是相关系数的平方；在这种情况下，$R^2 = 0.50^2 = 0.25$（确实，数据集的回归产生了精确的R-squared为0.2525，和调整后的R-squared为0.2478，两者都四舍五入为0.25）。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{多元回归结果的解释与多重共线性判断}{3}{2}

% --- Question 59 ---
\begin{question}
    Derivatives can be cleared through a central counterparty (CCP) or they can be cleared bilaterally; i.e., uncleared transactions are cleared bilaterally. If the derivatives are cleared through a CCP, they can be either exchange-traded or over-the-market (OTC). In this way, we distinguish between the exchange and the clearinghouse (which may be owned by an exchange). While exchanges operate CCPs, we can also refer to the emergent OTC CCPs. In regard to central counterparties (CCPs), which of the following statements is TRUE?
    \\衍生品可以通过中央对手方（CCP）清算，也可以通过双边清算；即，未清算的交易通过双边清算。如果衍生品通过CCP清算，它们可以是交易所交易（exchange-traded）或场外交易（OTC）。通过这种方式，我们可以区分交易所和清算所（可能由交易所拥有）。虽然交易所运营CCP，我们也可以指出现场场外CCP。关于中央对手方（CCPs），下列哪个陈述是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Banks are easier to regulate than both OTC CCP and exchange CCP
    \\银行比场外CCP和交易所CCP更容易监管
    \item An exchange CCP is more dependent on valuation models than an OTC CCP
    \\交易所CCP比场外CCP更依赖估值模型
    \item CCP member defaults are negatively correlated such that CCPs are counter-cyclical
    \\CCP成员违约是负相关的，因此CCP是反周期的
    \item In the cleared market, CCPs want initial margin that covers a 5-day $99.0\%$ price value at risk (VaR)
    \\在清算市场中，CCP希望初始保证金覆盖5天的$99.0\%$价格价值风险（VaR）
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}

    \textbf{A选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 银行比场外CCP和交易所CCP更容易监管
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 场外CCP比交易所CCP更依赖估值模型
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item CCP成员的违约是正相关的，因此CCP具有顺周期性
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 在清算市场中，CCP要求初始保证金能够覆盖5天内$99.0\%$的价格风险价值（VaR）。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{清算与结算：CCP 的监管与依赖模型}{3}{2}

% --- Question 60 ---
\begin{question}
    Exactly one year ago, Sally purchased a $\dollar100.00$ face value bond that pays a semi-annual coupon with a coupon rate of $9.0\%$ per annum. When she purchased the bond, it had a maturity of $10.0$ years and its yield to maturity (YTM; aka, yield) was $6.00\%$. If the bond's price today happens to be unchanged from one year ago (when she purchased the bond), which of the following is nearest to the bond's yield (yield to maturity) today?
    \\一年前，Sally 购买了一张面值为$\dollar100.00$的债券，每年支付两次$9.0\%$的利息。当她购买债券时，它的期限为10年，收益率（YTM；aka，收益率）为$6.00\%$。如果今天债券的价格与一年前相同（当她购买债券时），那么下列哪个选项最接近今天的债券收益率（yield to maturity）？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $5.57\%$
    \item $5.78\%$
    \item $6.00\%$
    \item $6.22\%$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 如果债券的价格保持不变，那么它的到期收益率是$5.78\%$，但它的期限缩短到$9.0$年。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 当Sally最初购买债券时，其价格为$=-PV(0.060/2, 10*2, \dollar100*0.090/2, 100) = \dollar122.3162$。如果价格保持不变，那么一年后（即$N - 2$期），收益率由$RATE(9*2, \dollar100*0.090/2, -122.3162, 100)*2 = 5.7843\%$给出。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 使用TI BA II+计算器：$20\ N, 3\ I/Y, 4.5\ PMT, 100\ FV$ 和 $CPT\ PV = -122.3162$，然后我们只需要改变期数为$18\ N, CPT\ I/Y$ [显示：$I/Y = 2.8922$] $\times 2 = 5.7843\%$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{债券收益率计算与久期影响}{3}{2}

% --- Question 61 ---
\begin{question}
    One of the risk management building blocks is the need to balance risk and reward. Specifically, GARP says, “Economic capital provides the firm with a conceptually satisfying way to balance risk and reward. For each activity, firms can compare the revenue and profit they are making from an activity to the amount of economic capital required to support that activity.” Each of the following statements is true about RAROC EXCEPT which is inaccurate?
    \\风险管理的一个关键组成部分是平衡风险和回报。具体来说，GARP 说：“经济资本提供了一种概念上令人满意的方式来平衡风险和回报。对于每个活动，公司可以比较从活动中获得的收入和利润与支持该活动的经济资本量。”下列每个陈述都是关于 RAROC 的，除了哪一项是不准确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item For an activity to increase shareholder value, its RAROC should be higher than the cost of equity capital
    \\为了增加股东价值，活动的RAROC应该高于股权资本的成本
    \item Four applications of RAROC include business comparison, investment analysis, pricing strategies, and risk management cost/benefit analysis
    \\RAROC的四个应用包括业务比较、投资分析、定价策略和风险管理成本/效益分析
    \item Advantages of RAROC include (i) it has one universal regulatory definition (without credible variants), such that benchmarking against peers is easy; and (ii) it is easy to implement in practice
    \\RAROC的优势包括（i）它有一个通用的监管定义（没有可信的变体），因此与同行比较很容易；和（ii）它在实践中很容易实施
    \item If RAROC's denominator is economic capital, which is typical, then its numerator should be an after-tax risk-adjusted expected return where the risk-adjusted refers to an adjustment for expected losses
    \\如果RAROC的分母是经济资本，这是典型的，那么它的分子应该是一个税后风险调整后的预期回报，其中风险调整指的是对预期损失的调整
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 若一项活动要提升股东价值，其RAROC应高于股权资本成本。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item RAROC的四个应用场景包括：业务比较、投资分析、定价策略和风险管理的成本/效益分析。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item C选项是错误的，并且有两方面原因。首先，RAROC（风险调整后资本回报率）因缺乏统一的定义且存在多种变体而广为诟病。正如GARP所说：“RAROC公式有很多变体，被应用于许多不同的行业和机构。”此外，RAROC在实际操作中并不容易：“在应用RAROC时存在许多实际困难，包括其对底层风险计算的依赖。业务线通常会出于自身利益质疑RAROC数据的有效性。”（引自GARP）。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D说法恰当，不选。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 如果RAROC的分母是经济资本（这很典型），那么其分子应为税后风险调整后的预期回报，其中“风险调整”指的是对预期损失的调整。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{RAROC 的定义与应用场景}{3}{2}

% --- Question 62 ---
\begin{question}
    Consider a random variable that represents the loss severity of a risky asset and is given by the continuous probability distribution $f(x) = 3x^2/64$ on the domain from zero to four:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·62.png} 
    \end{figure}
    What are this variable's mean and variance?
    \\考虑一个代表风险资产损失严重性的随机变量，由连续概率分布$f(x) = 3x^2/64$在零到四的域上给出：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·62.png} 
    \end{figure}
    这个变量的均值和方差是多少？
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item $\mu = 1.0$ and $\sigma^2 = 3.50$
        \item $\mu = 2.0$ and $\sigma^2 = 2.75$
        \item $\mu = 3.0$ and $\sigma^2 = 0.60$
        \item $\mu = 3.6$ and $\sigma^2 = 0.25$
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    C
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：$\mu = 3.0$ and $\sigma^2 = 0.60$
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 为了获取均值$\mu$，我们积分$x \cdot f(x)$
        \item 为了获取方差，我们积分$(x - \mu)^2 \cdot f(x)$
    \end{itemize}
    \end{explanation}
    
\checklist{连续概率分布的期望值与方差计算}{3}{2}

% --- Question 63 ---
\begin{question}
    A trader shorts 500 shares when the stock price is $\dollar70.00$. The initial and maintenance margin, respectively, are $150.0\%$ and $125.0\%$. At what share price will further margin be required?
    \\一个交易者以$\dollar70.00$的价格卖出500股股票。初始和维持保证金分别是150.0\%和125.0\%。在什么股票价格时需要进一步保证金？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $\dollar65.00$
    \item $\dollar72.00$
    \item $\dollar79.00$
    \item $\dollar84.00$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 因为初始保证金是$150.0\%$，交易者需要贡献$150.0\% \times 500 \times \dollar70.00 = \dollar52,500.00$，其中包含从卖出股票获得的$\dollar35,000.00$加上$\dollar17,500.00$。
        \item 如果股票价格增加到$X$，维持保证金变为$500 \times 1.25 \times X = 625 \times X$。当$625 \times X > \dollar52,500.00$时，即$X > \dollar52,500.00 / 625 = \dollar84.00$时，维持保证金大于$\dollar52,500.00$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{保证金交易的风险与回报平衡}{3}{2}

% --- Question 64 ---
\begin{question}
    As a market maker, Toughgreen Financial has written (aka, taken a short position in) a single contract for 100 call options on a non-dividend-paying stock whose volatility is $36.0\%$ per annum when the riskless rate is $3.0\%$. Their strike price is $\dollar100.00$ but the options are underwater because the current stock price is $\dollar90.00$. The option term is six months. At the current stock price, each option has a value of $\dollar5.88$ and each option's percentage delta, $\Delta = +0.410$. From Toughgreen's perspective, if the stock price increases by $+\dollar3.00$ to $\dollar93.00$, which is nearest to the impact on the position's value?
    \\作为一个做市商，Toughgreen Financial 以 $\dollar100.00$ 的执行价格写（aka，做空）了一份合约，合约是针对一个没有分红支付的股票的100份看涨期权。该股票的波动率为36.0\%，无风险利率为3.0\%。他们的执行价格是 $\dollar100.00$，但期权是亏本的，因为当前股票价格是 $\dollar90.00$。期权期限为六个月。在当前股票价格下，每个期权价值为 $\dollar5.88$，每个期权的百分比delta，$\Delta = +0.410$。从Toughgreen的角度来看，如果股票价格增加 $\dollar3.00$ 到 $\dollar93.00$，哪个最接近对头寸价值的影响？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item a) A loss of few dollars less than $\dollar8.00$; i.e., $100 * [\dollar5.88 * (\dollar3.00/\dollar90.00) * 0.410]$ minus (-) gamma adjustment
    \\a) 几美元的损失，小于 $\dollar8.00$；即，$100 * [\dollar5.88 * (\dollar3.00/\dollar90.00) * 0.410]$ 减去 (-) gamma 调整
    \item b) A loss of few dollars more than $\dollar8.00$; i.e., $100 * [\dollar5.88 * (\dollar3.00/\dollar90.00) * 0.410]$ plus (+) gamma adjustment
    \\b) 几美元的损失，大于 $\dollar8.00$；即，$100 * [\dollar5.88 * (\dollar3.00/\dollar90.00) * 0.410]$ 加上 (+) gamma 调整
    \item c) A loss of few dollars less than $\dollar123.00$; i.e., $100 * (\dollar3.00 * 0.410)$ minus (-) gamma adjustment
    \\c) 几美元的损失，小于 $\dollar123.00$；即，$100 * (\dollar3.00 * 0.410)$ 减去 (-) gamma 调整
    \item d) A loss of few dollars more than $\dollar123.00$; i.e., $100 * (\dollar3.00 * 0.410)$ plus (+) gamma adjustment
    \\d) 几美元的损失，大于 $\dollar123.00$；即，$100 * (\dollar3.00 * 0.410)$ 加上 (+) gamma 调整
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 损失会比$\dollar123.00$多几美元；即，$100 \times (\dollar3.00 \times 0.410)$，再加上（+）gamma 调整项。
    \end{itemize}

    \begin{itemize}
        \item Delta 是期权价格对标的资产（本题为股票）价格变化的一阶导数：$\Delta = \partial c/ \partial S$，即期权价格对标的变化的敏感度。百分比 delta 为 $0.410$ 表示股票价格每变化 $\dollar1.00$，看涨期权的价格期望线性变化 $\dollar0.410$。不过这只考虑了 delta，不含 gamma 的影响：实际上，期权价格的涨幅会大于线性近似值，因此空头（写出期权方）面临的损失会更大。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 具体到本题（虽然题目并未要求精确计算 gamma），百分比 gamma 大约为 $0.0170$，因此股票上涨 $+\dollar3.00$ 导致的损失为 $\dollar3.00\times0.410 + 0.5\times0.0170\times(\dollar3.00)^2 = \dollar1.3065$。事实上，期权价格从 $\dollar5.882$ 上涨到 $\dollar7.188$，实际涨幅为 $\dollar1.3060$。因此该头寸（100份期权）的实际损失是 $\dollar130.60$，而 delta-gamma 估算值是 $\dollar130.65$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{期权 Delta 与 Gamma 对头寸损益的影响}{3}{2}

% --- Question 65 ---
\begin{question}
    According to GARP, one of the building blocks in risk management is a proper understanding of the difference between expected loss, unexpected loss, and extreme risk; aka, tail risk. In regard to this building block, which of the following statements is TRUE?
    \\根据GARP，风险管理的一个关键组成部分是正确理解预期损失、意外损失和极端风险（aka，尾部风险）之间的区别。关于这个关键组成部分，下列哪个陈述是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Effective risk management should reduce a credit portfolio's expected loss (EL) to approximately zero
    \\有效的风险管理应该将信用组合的预期损失（EL）减少到大约零
    \item Expected loss is a product of (i) the probability of the risk event occurring; (ii) the severity of the loss if the risk event occurs, and (iii) the expected recovery rate
    \\预期损失是一个产品，包括（i）风险事件发生的概率；（ii）风险事件发生时的损失严重程度；和（iii）预期回收率
    \item While expected loss (EL) is a function of default correlation, unexpected loss (UL) is NOT influenced by portfolio granularity
    \\虽然预期损失（EL）是违约相关性的函数，但意外损失（UL）不受投资组合粒度影响
    \item Although banks theoretically do not need to set aside provisions when loan products are accurately priced, in realistic practice, banks should provision for expected losses
    \\尽管理论上银行在贷款产品准确定价时不需要计提准备金，但在实际实践中，银行应该计提预期损失
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    
    \textbf{A选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 所有信用组合都有非负的预期损失（EL）
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{B选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 预期损失是（i）风险事件发生的概率；（ii）风险事件发生时的损失严重程度；和（iii）违约时的暴露。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{C选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 虽然意外损失（UL）是违约相关性的函数，但预期损失（EL）不受投资组合粒度影响
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：尽管银行在贷款产品准确定价时理论上不需要计提准备金，但在实际实践中，银行应该计提预期损失。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{预期损失、意外损失与准备金计提}{3}{2}

% --- Question 66 ---
\begin{question}
    Below is a scatterplot of monthly returns, Fund A versus Fund B, over twenty months ($n = 20$).
    
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·66·1.png} 
    \end{figure}
    
    Skewness is a trivial (special) case of coskewness; e.g., $S(AAA)$ and $S(BBB)$ are the trivial instances of coskewness. Similarly, Kurtosis is the trivial case of coskewness; e.g., $K(AAAA)$ and $K(BBBB)$ are the trivial instances of cokurtosis. In regard to this scatterplot (this two-variable application), how many nontrivial coskewness measures apply; are they positive or negative; and how many nontrivial cokurtosis measures exist?

    下图展示了A基金与B基金在二十个月（月度回报，$n = 20$）中的散点图。
    
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·66·1.png} 
    \end{figure}

    偏度（Skewness）是共偏度（coskewness）的一个特殊（平凡）情况，例如$S(AAA)$和$S(BBB)$就是共偏度的平凡实例。同样地，峰度（Kurtosis）是共峰度（cokurtosis）的平凡情况，例如$K(AAAA)$和$K(BBBB)$是共峰度的平凡实例。针对本题中的散点图（两变量的应用），满足多少个非平凡共偏度？它们是正的还是负的？非平凡共峰度又有多少个？
    
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item There are two nontrivial coskewness measures and they are negative (aka, negative coskewness); there are three nontrivial cokurtosis measures
        \\有两项非平凡共偏度，它们是负的（aka，负共偏度）；有三项非平凡共峰度
        \item There are three nontrivial coskewness measures and they are negative (aka, negative coskewness); there are four nontrivial cokurtosis measures
        \\有三项非平凡共偏度，它们是负的（aka，负共偏度）；有四项非平凡共峰度
        \item There are two nontrivial coskewness measures and they are positive (aka, positive coskewness); there are five nontrivial cokurtosis measures
        \\有两项非平凡共偏度，它们是正的（aka，正共偏度）；有五项非平凡共峰度
        \item There are three nontrivial coskewness measures and they are positive (aka, positive coskewness); there are six nontrivial cokurtosis measures
        \\有三项非平凡共偏度，它们是正的（aka，正共偏度）；有六项非平凡共峰度
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    A
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 有两项非平凡共偏度，它们是负的（aka，负共偏度）；有三项非平凡共峰度
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 当变量有两个时，存在两项非平凡的共偏度（coskewness）矩和三项非平凡的共峰度（cokurtosis）矩。对于偏度，共矩包括：$S(AAA)$、$S(AAB)$、$S(ABB)$ 和 $S(BBB)$，其中 $S(AAA)$ 和 $S(BBB)$ 是（平凡的）单变量偏度矩。
        \item 对于共峰度，非平凡的共矩包括 $K(AAAB)$、$K(AABB)$ 和 $K(ABBB)$。
        \item 从图形上来看，这对基金之间呈现负共偏度，因为它们的负收益往往发生在同一期间（参见散点图左下象限，关系较为线性），而它们的正收益则往往在不同期间出现（参见右上象限，点分布更为分散）。如果将两只基金合并成一个投资组合，它们的负共偏度反映出当收益为负时，风险相对更高（相较于收益为正时表现更为分散的情形）。
    \end{itemize}

    如果你想进行更深入的探索，下面是相关电子表格链接（但对于本次考试，这部分理解不是必需的！）：
    https://www.dropbox.com/s/i7bx2iewfrezgph/t2-20-13-3-sample-moments-3rd-coskewness.xlsx
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·66·2.png} 
    \end{figure}
    \end{explanation}
    
\checklist{共偏度与共峰度（高阶矩）}{3}{2}



% --- Question 67 ---
\begin{question}
    Ralph creates a portfolio with two positions: a short call and a short put with identical maturities on the same stock that has a current price, $S(0) = \dollar20.00$. His short call with a strike price, $K_1 = \dollar22.00$ has a premium, $c_1 = \dollar1.75$. His short put with a strike price, $K_2 = \dollar18.00$ has a premium, $p_2 = \dollar1.25$. Both options are out-of-the-money by the same amount of $\dollar2.00$. Recall that option profit is equal to the payoff net of the premium and we (typically) ignore the impact of discounting. On the future date with both options mature, under which future stock price scenario is Ralph’s trade profitable?
    \\Ralph 创建了一个投资组合，包括两个头寸：一个短卖看涨期权和一个短卖看跌期权，这两个期权具有相同的到期日，并且针对同一只股票，当前价格为 $S(0) = \dollar20.00$。他的短卖看涨期权，执行价格为 $K_1 = \dollar22.00$，期权费为 $c_1 = \dollar1.75$。他的短卖看跌期权，执行价格为 $K_2 = \dollar18.00$，期权费为 $p_2 = \dollar1.25$。这两个期权都处于相同的金额，即 $\dollar2.00$ 的虚值。记住，期权利润等于净收益减去期权费，我们（通常）忽略折现的影响。在未来日期，当两个期权都到期时，哪个未来股票价格场景使得 Ralph 的交易盈利？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item If the stock price falls within $\dollar15.00$ and $\dollar25.00$
    \\如果股票价格在 $\dollar15.00$ 和 $\dollar25.00$ 之间
    \item His portfolio will always generate a small profit
    \\他的投资组合总是会产生小额利润
    \item If the stock price is less than $\dollar18.00$ or greater than $\dollar22.00$
    \\如果股票价格小于 $\dollar18.00$ 或大于 $\dollar22.00$
    \item If realized volatility increases; i.e., is higher on the future date
    \\如果实际波动率增加；即，在未来日期更高
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 该投资组合是一种被称为“空跨式套利”（short strangle）或“顶部垂直组合”的交易策略，这是比常见的跨式（straddle）更加变体的一种方式。在跨式策略中，投资者同时买入（欧式）看涨期权和看跌期权，二者有相同的行权价和到期日。这不是方向性交易，而是一种波动性交易，因为只要股价大幅上涨或下跌就能获利。strangle（宽跨式）则是其中的一个变体，因为在strangle中，看涨期权的行权价高于看跌期权的行权价。本题所示的写strangle（卖出宽跨式）是一种做空波动率的交易，因为一旦股价大幅上涨或下跌，就可能遭受亏损。具体来说，初始现金流入（两笔权利金）为$\dollar1.75 + \dollar1.25 = \dollar3.00$。因此盈亏平衡点出现在期末损益等于$\dollar3.00$的点位：股价低于$\dollar15.00$（此时被行权的看跌期权带来的损失为$\dollar3.00$）或者高于$\dollar25.00$（此时被行权的看涨期权带来的损失为$\dollar3.00$）。所以这个空宽跨式策略的股票价格在$\{\dollar15.00, \dollar25.00\}$之间时能获得净利润。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于错误选项（D），买入跨式（又称底部跨式，bottom straddle）是一种做多波动率的策略，而卖出跨式（又称顶部跨式，top straddle）是一种做空波动率的策略。由于本题中两个行权价不同，组合属于空宽跨式，与卖出跨式类似，因为它是做空波动率策略：如果实际波动率低于初始隐含波动率则盈利；如果实际波动率大于初始隐含波动率则会亏损。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{卖出宽跨式与做空波动率}{3}{2}

% --- Question 68 ---
\begin{question}
    Sally owns a non-dividend-paying stock that is currently trading at $\dollar60.00$. As a hedge, she buys an out-of-the-money three-month European put option on the stock with a fixed strike price of $\dollar57.00$, which is a $5.0\%$ discount to the current stock price. The stock’s volatility is $36.0\%$ per annum. The riskfree rate is $4.0\%$. According to the Black-Scholes-Merton model, what is the value of the put?
    \\Sally 拥有一只不支付股息的股票，目前交易价格为 $\dollar60.00$。为了对冲，她购买了一个虚值三个月的欧式看跌期权，行权价为 $\dollar57.00$，这是当前股价的 $5.0\%$ 折扣。股票的波动率为 $36.0\%$ 年化。无风险利率为 $4.0\%$。根据 Black-Scholes-Merton 模型，认沽期权的价值是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $\dollar1.55$
    \item $\dollar2.63$
    \item $\dollar3.89$
    \item $\dollar4.40$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 按照BSM（Black-Scholes-Merton）期权定价模型，认沽期权的价值公式为：$p = K \times \exp(-rT) \times N(-d_2) - S(0) \times N(-d_1)$。
        \item 如果采用四舍五入查表值得到：$p = \dollar57.00 \times \exp(-0.040 \times 3/12) \times 0.4013 - \dollar60.00 \times 0.336 = \dollar2.630$。
        \item 实际上，这也是认沽期权的精确价值（即使用正态分布确切Z值时），因为 $c = \dollar57.00 \times \exp(-0.040 \times 3/12) \times 0.4011 - \dollar60.00 \times 0.334 = \dollar2.630$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{BSM 模型下认沽期权的定价}{3}{2}

% --- Question 69 ---
\begin{question}
    Galaxy Financial is a large bank whose risk department developed the following risk typology after surveying key stakeholders within the bank:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·69.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Which of the following is most likely TRUE about their typology?
    \\Galaxy Financial 是一个大型银行，其风险部门在调查银行内部的关键利益相关者后开发了以下风险分类：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·69.png} 
    \end{figure}
    下列哪个陈述最可能是关于他们的分类的？
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item This typology is incorrect because it does not comport with any of the typologies approved by regulators, including Basel
        \\这个分类是错误的，因为它不符合监管机构批准的任何分类，包括巴塞尔协议
        \item If the typology is good then any and all newly identified risks should be slotted into an existing risk type within the typology
        \\如果分类是好的，那么所有新识别的风险都应该被放入分类中的现有风险类型
        \item Although not illustrated directly here, many of the risk types interact with one another so that risk might flow or cascade from one type to another
        \\尽管这里没有直接说明，但许多风险类型相互作用，因此风险可能从一个类型流向另一个类型
        \item It is incorrect to locate Liquidity risk as a top-level risk (i.e., alongside Credit and Market risk) because it should be a sub-risk to operational risk
        \\将流动性风险定位为顶级风险（即，与信用风险和市场风险并列）是错误的，因为它应该是操作风险的一个子风险
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    C
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    

    \textbf{A选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 我们不能仅凭有限的信息就判断这种分类法是错误的，因为没有所谓唯一的“正确”分类法；监管机构也不会要求银行必须采用某一特定分类法。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{B选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 风险分类法是动态的而非静态的。新的风险（如网络风险）会创造新的风险类型，或使当前类型进一步细分。GARP解释道：“风险分类法必须具有灵活性，因为新风险始终在不断出现。”[1]
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 尽管在分类中没有直接说明，但许多风险类型相互作用，因此风险可能从一个类型流向另一个类型
        \item 如GARP所解释：“风险类型彼此之间是相互作用的，因此风险是会流动的。例如，在严重的危机期间，风险可以从信用风险流向流动性风险，再流向市场风险（如2007–2009年全球金融危机时的情形）。同样的现象也可能在单个公司内部发生：某个交易员的误操作（操作风险）会造成危险的市场头寸（市场风险），并有可能损害公司的声誉地位（声誉风险）。这就是为什么对风险类型及其相互作用有深入理解，是风险管理的重要基础。”[1]
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 从灵活性的角度看，流动性风险可以是顶层风险，也可以是市场风险的子风险。有趣的是，2020年GARP在FRM第二部分引入了“流动性与资金风险的衡量和管理”作为新的顶级主题。此前，流动性风险在某种程度上是属于操作风险主题的。
    \end{itemize}

    [1] 2020 FRM Part I: Foundations of Risk Management, 10th Edition. Pearson Learning Solutions, 10/2019
    \end{explanation}
    
\checklist{风险分类法与风险类型的相互作用}{3}{2}

% --- Question 70 ---
\begin{question}
    An asset has a one-month volatility of 6.0\%. If the asset's returns are i.i.d., the asset has a three-month volatility of $6.0\% \times \sqrt{3/1} = 10.4\%$ and a one-year volatility of $6.0\% \times \sqrt{12/1} = 20.8\%$. In general, by convention (and to avoid confusion), we should prefer the annualized measure: interest rate and volatility inputs and outputs are generally expressed in per annum terms. However, let's assume we are interested in the three-month value-at-risk (VaR) of this asset. If we assume that the asset's arithmetic returns, $[S(t)-S(t-1)]/S(t-1)$, have a normal distribution and i.i.d., then per the square root rule (SRR) the 95.0\% confident three-month relative VaR of this asset is $6.0\% \times \sqrt{3/1} \times 1.645 = 17.10\%$; the ``relative'' adjective signifies that we are excluding the positive expected return which would mitigate this worst expected loss.

    Now let's violate the i.i.d. assumption. Specifically, let's retain the ``identical'' but edit the ``independence'' to make an assumption of +0.50 auto- or serial-correlation between the returns; i.e., if the returns are correlated, they cannot be independent and i.i.d. is not satisfied such that we cannot rely on the square root rule (SRR). Now we are interested in the three-period (i.e., three-month) VaR based on a three-month volatility but where the variable (log return) has a positive correlation (with itself) of 0.50. Hint: per variance properties $\sigma^2(X + Y + Z) = \sigma^2(X + Y) + \sigma^2(Z) + 2 \times \text{COV}(X +Y, Z)$ or equivalently $\sigma^2(R1 + R2 + R3) = \sigma^2(R1 + R2) + \sigma^2(R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R1 +R2, R3)$.

    If each one-month return has a standard deviation of 6.0\% and the serial correlation between returns is +0.50, which is \textbf{nearest} to the three-month 95.0\% normal relative VaR?

    一项资产的单月波动率为6.0\%。如果该资产的收益是i.i.d.的（一致且独立同分布），则该资产的三个月波动率为$6.0\% \times \sqrt{3/1} = 10.4\%$，一年期波动率为$6.0\% \times \sqrt{12/1} = 20.8\%$。一般来说，按照惯例（并且为了避免混淆），我们应优先采用年化指标：利率和波动率的输入与输出通常以每年为单位表示。然而，假设我们关心该资产的三个月价值-at-风险（VaR）。如果我们假定资产的算术收益$[S(t)-S(t-1)]/S(t-1)$服从正态分布且i.i.d.，则根据平方根法则（SRR），该资产在95.0\%置信三个月的相对VaR为$6.0\% \times \sqrt{3/1} \times 1.645 = 17.10\%$；“相对”一词意味着我们未计入正的预期收益，这些预期收益会缓和这种最坏预期损失。

    现在我们违反i.i.d.假设。具体来说，我们保留“一致性”，但调整“独立性”，假设收益之间的自相关（序列相关）为+0.50；即如果收益相关，它们就不是独立的，此时不满足i.i.d.假设，因此不能依赖平方根法则（SRR）。现在我们关心基于三个月波动率且变量（对数收益）自相关为0.50情况下的三期（即三个月）VaR。提示：根据方差性质$\sigma^2(X + Y + Z) = \sigma^2(X + Y) + \sigma^2(Z) + 2 \times \text{COV}(X +Y, Z)$，或等价地$\sigma^2(R1 + R2 + R3) = \sigma^2(R1 + R2) + \sigma^2(R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R1 +R2, R3)$。

    如果每个月的标准差为6.0\%，且月收益之间的序列相关系数为+0.50，哪一个最接近于三个月期95.0\%正态分布下的相对VaR？
    
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item 17.1\%
    \item 24.2\%
    \item 29.6\%
    \item 51.5\%
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 采用$\sigma^2(R1 + R2 + R3) = \sigma^2(R1 + R2) + \sigma^2(R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R1 +R2, R3)$的递推公式，进一步展开：

        \item 因为 $2 \times \text{COV}(R1 +R2, R3) = 2 \times [\text{COV}(R1, R3) + \text{COV}(R2, R3)] = 2 \times \text{COV}(R1, R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R2, R3)$，代入得到：

        \item $\sigma^2(R1 + R2 + R3) = \sigma^2(R1) + \sigma^2(R2) + \sigma^2(R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R1,R2) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R1, R3) + 2 \times \text{COV}(R2, R3)$；这是三变量方差公式。
        \item 根据“一致性”假设，$\sigma^2(R1) = \sigma^2(R2) = \sigma^2(R3) = 6.0\%^2$；且$\rho(R1,R2) = \rho(R1,R3) = \rho(R2,R3) = 0.50$，因此：
        \item $\sigma^2(R1 + R2 + R3) = 3 \times 6\%^2 + 3 \times 2 \times 6\% \times 6\% \times 0.5 = 0.02160$，因此$\sigma(R1 + R2 + R3) = \sqrt{0.02160} = 14.70\%$
        \item 三个月95.0\%置信水平下，正态相对VaR为$14.70\% \times 1.645 = 24.18\%$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{多期 VaR 与序列相关下的方差（平方根法则的适用条件）}{3}{2}

% --- Question 71 ---
\begin{question}
    Joe is a 29-year-old single male who prefers to invest in a fund because he has neither the time nor interest to spend researching and selecting individual stocks. His financial advisor Sally is experience in the following fund categories: closed- and open-end mutual funds, hedge funds, and index funds. When Sally asks for Joe’s preferences, his only strongly felt opinion is that he prefers to avoid paying high expenses or fees (reading the financial news gives him the impression that he should seek low fees because fees are trending toward zero). Which of the following is she MOST LIKELY to recommend to Joe?

    Joe 是一个29岁的单身男性，他更喜欢投资基金，因为他没有时间和兴趣去研究并选择单个股票。他的财务顾问 Sally 在以下基金类别方面有经验：封闭式和开放式共同基金、对冲基金和指数基金。当 Sally 询问 Joe 的偏好时，他唯一的强烈感受是，他更喜欢避免支付高额费用或费用（阅读金融新闻使他相信，由于费用趋向于零，他应该寻求低费用）。下列哪一项是她最有可能推荐给 Joe 的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item An index fund (or exchange-traded fund, ETF) to achieve diversification with low fees
    \\一个指数基金（或交易所交易基金，ETF），以实现多元化并控制费用
    \item An active closed-end mutual fund that trades below its net asset value (NAV) because it will outperform as it pulls to maturity
    \\一个活跃的封闭式共同基金，其交易价格低于净资产价值（NAV），因为它将在到期时表现出色
    \item A fund of hedge funds (FOHF) in order to overcome the principal-agent problem and ensure alignment between Joe’s interest and the managers
    \\一个对冲基金的基金（FOHF），以克服主要-代理问题并确保 Joe 的利益与经理之间的对齐
    \item An open-end mutual fund that has beaten the market in the prior three consecutive years because mutual fund performance tends to be persistent
    \\一个开放式共同基金，在过去三年中击败了市场，因为共同基金的表现倾向于持续
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 一个指数基金（或交易所交易基金，ETF），以实现多元化并控制费用
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 封闭式基金不一定“拉平”：封闭式基金倾向于以折价交易
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 对冲基金的基金（FOHF）有两层费用（众所周知非常昂贵！），不会满足 Joe 希望保持低费用的偏好
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 过去的表现不能保证未来的表现，不幸的是，共同基金的表现往往不持续。根据 GARP（参见“共同基金研究”），主动管理的基金平均表现不如市场；并且研究已经发现，\textit{持续性}是广大共同基金中罕见的特征。
    \end{itemize}

\end{explanation}

\checklist{指数基金与费用控制}{3}{2}

% --- Question 72 ---
\begin{question}
    Given a universe of 500 stocks and an initial assumption for the risk-free rate, an analyst generates the efficient frontier. She assumes the mean-variance framework. The investment committee compliments her analysis and makes a request: they ask her to decrease the risk-free rate (the rate at which an investor can both borrow and lend) but otherwise make no changes (aka, ceteris paribus) to the expected returns or variance matrix for the equities. Which of the following is most likely to be correct?
    \\给定500只股票的 universe 和一个初始的无风险利率假设，分析师生成有效前沿。她假设均值-方差框架。投资委员会补充了她的分析，并提出了一个请求：他们要求她降低无风险利率（投资者可以借贷和放贷的利率），但除此之外不做任何改变（aka，ceteris paribus），即对股票的预期回报或方差矩阵不做任何改变。下列哪一项最可能是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item The expected return on optimal portfolios with low risk \& high risk will both decrease
    \\低风险和高风险的最优投资组合的预期回报都会减少
    \item The expected return on optimal portfolios with low risk will decrease, and the expected return on optimal portfolios with high risk will increase
    \\低风险的最优投资组合的预期回报会减少，而高风险的最优投资组合的预期回报会增加
    \item The expected return on optimal portfolios with low risk will increase, and the expected return on optimal portfolios with high risk will decrease
    \\低风险的最优投资组合的预期回报会增加，而高风险的最优投资组合的预期回报会减少
    \item The expected return on optimal portfolios with low risk \& high risk will both increase
    \\低风险和高风险的最优投资组合的预期回报都会增加
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：低风险最优投资组合的预期回报会减少，而高风险最优投资组合的预期回报会增加
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 观察以下图表：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·72.png}
    \end{figure}
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 随着无风险利率下降，新的有效前沿线现在有一个较低的截距和较高的斜率——因此，追求理性策略的投资者如果选择非常低风险的投资组合，将获得较低的回报；但如果他们选择在风险维度上更加激进（标准差），则将获得更高的回报。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{资本市场线与风险偏好变化}{3}{2}

% --- Question 73 ---
\begin{question}
    In a single-factor economy, each of the following portfolios (A, B, and C) is well-diversified:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
            \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·73.png}
    \end{figure}
    You discover there is NOT an arbitrage strategy among these three portfolios. In this case, what should be the expected return of Portfolio (C)?
    \\在一个单因子经济中，以下每个投资组合（A、B和C）都是充分分散的：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·73.png}
    \end{figure}
    你发现这三个投资组合之间不存在套利策略。在这种情况下，投资组合C的预期收益率应该是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item 13.3\%
    \item 16.3\%
    \item 18.5\%
    \item 21.0\%
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 所有三个投资组合都必须位于相同的SML上，因此投资组合C的Treynor比率必须与其他投资组合相同。
        \item Treynor(Portfolio A) = $(12.0\%-3.0\%)/0.60 = 0.15$; Treynor(Portfolio B) = $(15.0\%-3.0\%)/0.80 = 0.15$，也是。因此，Treynor(Portfolio C) = $(R - 3.0\%)/1.20 = 0.15$，因此其回报必须为21.0\%。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{单因子模型下无套利与 Treynor 比率（SML）}{3}{4}

% --- Question 74 ---
\begin{question}
    Let's assume two normal variables: $X \sim N(0, 3.0^2)$ and $Y \sim N(4.0, 9.0^2)$. Let $V$ be a mixture distribution where $X$ has a component weight of $80\%$ and $Y$ has a component weight of $20\%$. Let $W = 0.80*X + 0.20*Y$ with the assumption that $X$ and $Y$ are independent; that is, this $W$ is the sum of independent, random variables. Which is more likely (the mixture or the convolution) to realize a negative outcome; that is, is $\text{Pr}(V<0) > \text{Pr}(W<0)$, or on the other hand, is $\text{Pr}(W<0) > \text{Pr}(V<0)$?
    \\假设两个正态分布变量：$X \sim N(0, 3.0^2)$ 和 $Y \sim N(4.0, 9.0^2)$。令 $V$ 为一个混合分布，其中 $X$ 的成分权重为80\%，$Y$ 的成分权重为20\%。令 $W = 0.80*X + 0.20*Y$，假设 $X$ 和 $Y$ 是独立的；即，$W$ 是独立随机变量的和。哪一种更可能实现负结果；即，$\text{Pr}(V<0) > \text{Pr}(W<0)$，还是$\text{Pr}(W<0) > \text{Pr}(V<0)$？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item The mixture (V) has a greater probability of a negative outcome; i.e., $\text{Pr}(V<0) > \text{Pr}(W<0)$
    \\混合分布（V）有更大的概率实现负结果；即，$\text{Pr}(V<0) > \text{Pr}(W<0)$
    \item The convolution (W) has a greater probability of a negative outcome; i.e., $\text{Pr}(W<0) > \text{Pr}(V<0)$
    \\卷积（W）有更大的概率实现负结果；即，$\text{Pr}(W<0) > \text{Pr}(V<0)$
    \item They have the same probability of realizing a negative outcome; i.e., $\text{Pr}(W<0) = \text{Pr}(V<0)$
    \\它们有相同的可能性实现负结果；即，$\text{Pr}(W<0) = \text{Pr}(V<0)$
    \item It cannot be answered because it depends on correlation in the case of (W)
    \\它无法回答，因为它取决于卷积（W）的情况中的相关性
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 混合分布（V）有更大的概率实现负值；即，$\text{Pr}(V<0) > \text{Pr}(W<0)$
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于混合分布，$\text{Pr}(V < 0) = 80\%*50.0\% + 20\%*32.84\% = 40.0\% + 6.567\% = 46.57\%$。
        \item 关于卷积（即，独立随机变量的和，根据给定假设），W的均值为$0.80$，标准差为$\sqrt{0.80^2*3.0^2 + 0.20^2*9.0^2} = 3.0$，因此$\text{Pr}(W < 0) = 39.49\%$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{混合分布与卷积分布的概率计算}{3}{2}

% --- Question 75 ---
\begin{question}
    Customers of insurance companies do not like to see their premiums increase, obviously. At the same time, most customers care about the claims process and quality: if the insurance doesn’t pay out when it is needed, so the cheapest policy is not always the best! In regard to an increase in the premium(s), which of the following is MOST LIKELY to be TRUE?
    \\保险公司的客户不喜欢看到他们的保费增加，显然。同时，大多数客户关心索赔过程和质量：如果保险在需要时没有支付，那么最便宜的策略并不总是最好的！关于保费的增加，哪一项最可能是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item A whole life insurance premium increases in the middle of the policy because the insurance company’s shareholders have demanded an increase in the dividend
    \\一个终身保险的保费在政策中间增加，因为保险公司的股东要求增加股息
    \item A variable life insurance premium increases because new genetic information (i.e., family tree insights) reduces the policy holder’s life expectancy
    \\一个变额终身保险的保费增加，因为新的遗传信息（即家族树洞察）减少了政策持有人的预期寿命
    \item A health insurance premium increases due to a higher cost for physician services, new technologies and/or hospital upgrades
    \\一个健康保险的保费增加，因为医生服务的成本更高，新的技术和/或医院升级
    \item A health insurance premium increases because the policyholder develops unexpected health problems that were unanticipated when the policy was written
    \\一个健康保险的保费增加，因为政策持有人在政策写作时未预料到的发展意外健康问题
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 一个健康保险的保费增加，因为医生服务的成本更高，新的技术和/或医院升级。
        \item 人寿保险公司不能在政策期间增加保费；他们可以在政策续保时增加保费
        \item 平价医疗法案修改了对预先存在条件的处理（https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pre-existing\_condition）。预先存在条件的问题是，保险公司面临逆向选择，限制了他们正确分配风险的能力。然而，政策制定后出现健康问题正是保险的用途！
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{保险类型与保费变动原因（逆选择与预先存在条件）}{3}{2}

% --- Question 76 ---
\begin{question}
    In the wake of the global financial crisis (GFC), stress testing has received prominent media attention. Siddique and Hasan explain that ``stress testing has many approaches. For example, at many institutions, finance and treasury have had ownership of the budgeting process. Hence, centralized stress testing could be housed in such a central function. Other institutions have taken a more decentralized approach, wherein the central function has only acted as an aggregator. Effective stress testing does not need a given organisational form or approach. However, a given organisational form will require certain aspects to ensure effectiveness. For example, in a decentralized approach towards stress testing, consistency across the organisation becomes important, and the institution will need to find ways of ensuring that consistency.''[1]

    Each of the following statements is true about stress testing EXCEPT which is false?

    [1] Siddique and Hasan, Stress Testing: Approaches, Methods, and Applications (London: Risk Books, 2013)

    在金融危机（GFC）之后，压力测试受到了媒体的高度关注。Siddique 和 Hasan 解释说，“压力测试有多种方法。例如，在许多机构中，财务和 Treasury 拥有预算过程的所有权。因此，集中化的压力测试可以放在这样一个中心职能中。其他机构采取了更加分散的方法，即中心职能只充当一个聚合器。有效的压力测试不需要特定的组织形式或方法。然而，特定的组织形式需要确保某些方面是有效的。例如，在分散的压力测试方法中，组织的一致性变得重要，机构需要找到方法来确保这种一致性。”[1]
    
    下列每个陈述都是关于压力测试的，除了哪一个是错误的？

    [1] Siddique 和 Hasan，压力测试：方法和应用（伦敦：风险图书，2013年）
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Stress testing is a non-statistical risk measure that may employ scenario analysis as one approach
    \\压力测试是一种非统计风险度量，可能采用情景分析作为一种方法
    \item An acceptable proxy for stress testing is to generate value at risk (VaR) with a series of progressively increasing confidence levels; e.g., $99.0\%$, $99.50\%$, $99.75\%$, $99.90\%$, and $99.99\%$
    \\一个可接受的代理压力测试是生成一系列逐渐增加的置信水平的价值风险（VaR）；即，$99.0\%$，$99.50\%$，$99.75\%$，$99.90\%$，和$99.99\%$
    \item While backtesting validates model parameter(s) using a large number of actual, historical observations; stress testing on the other hand uses a small number of extreme movements in market variables
    \\回溯测试使用大量实际的历史观察来验证模型参数；另一方面，压力测试使用市场变量中的少量极端运动
    \item Unlike value at risk (VaR) which indicates neither the magnitude nor shape of extreme losses beyond the VaR threshold, stress testing DOES attempt to characterize the magnitude/shape of the extreme loss tail
    \\与价值风险（VaR）不同，它既不指示极端损失的大小，也不指示极端损失的形状超过VaR阈值，压力测试确实试图表征极端损失尾部的大小/形状
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 该说法是错误的，或者至少不能说高置信水平的VaR系列能很好地替代压力测试。VaR可能基于历史观测数据，而这些数据未必能预测（或设想）不连续的市场剧烈变化。与之相关的原因也导致本题(D)选项的说法为真，因此该说法同样是错误的。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{压力测试与 VaR 的区别及尾部风险刻画}{3}{2}

% --- Question 77 ---
\begin{question}
    Suppose that there are two independent economic factors, F1 and F2. The risk-free rate is 1.0\%, and all stocks have independent firm-specific components with a standard deviation of 25\%. The following are well-diversified portfolios; e.g., Portfolio (A) has a beta sensitivity to factor the first factor, $\beta(F1)$, of 1.20 and an expected return of 13.0\%:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·77.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Which is the correct return-beta relationship in this economy?
    假设有两个独立的经济因素，F1 和 F2。无风险利率为1.0\%，所有股票都有独立的特定公司成分，标准差为25\%。以下是充分分散的投资组合；例如，投资组合（A）对第一个因素F1的敏感度为1.20，预期回报为13.0\%：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·77.png} 
    \end{figure}
    在这个经济体中，哪个是正确的回报-beta关系？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $E[R(P)] = 1.0\% - \beta(F1)*6.0\% - \beta(F2)*4.0\%$
    \item $E[R(P)] = 1.0\% - \beta(F1)*5.0\% + \beta(F2)*2.0\%$
    \item $E[R(P)] = 1.0\% + \beta(F1)*9.0\% + \beta(F2)*3.0\%$
    \item $E[R(P)] = 1.0\% + \beta(F1)*12.0\% + \beta(F2)*8.0\%$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $E[R(P)] = 1.0\% + \beta(F1)*12.0\% + \beta(F2)*8.0\%$。
        \item 我们需要解决两个方程，两个未知数：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item $0.13 = 0.01 + 1.20*RP(1) - 0.30*RP(2)$
            \item $0.05 = 0.01 + 0.60*RP(1) - 0.40*RP(2)$
        \end{itemize}
        \item 方法是将第二个方程乘以2，然后从第一个方程中减去，以消除RP(1)：
        $$0.13 = 0.01 + 1.20*RP(1) - 0.30*RP(2)$$
        $$- 2*[0.05 = 0.01 + 0.60*RP(1) - 0.40*RP(2)]$$
        $$= 0.03 = -0.01 + 0.5*RP(2) \rightarrow RP(2) = 0.04/0.5 = 0.08.$$
    \end{itemize}
    
   
\end{explanation}

\checklist{多因子模型回报-β关系与因子风险溢价}{3}{2}

% --- Question 78 ---
\begin{question}
    The scatterplot below plots sixty pairwise monthly returns over the last five years; each dot represents the return of Y (on the y-axis) and the return of X (on the x-axis) in a given month. Also shown is the ordinary least squares (OLS) regression line generated by Excel: $Y = 1.50 \times X + 0.78$. Finally, the univariate variances for each series are shown.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·78.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Which of the following is nearest to the correlation between X and Y? (Bonus question: if we flipped the axes, then what is the correlation?)

    下图展示了过去五年内六十对月度回报的散点图；每个点代表给定月份中Y的回报（在y轴上）和X的回报（在x轴上）。还显示了Excel生成的普通最小二乘（OLS）回归线：$Y = 1.50 \times X + 0.78$。最后，每个系列的单变量方差也显示了。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·78.png} 
    \end{figure}
    下列哪个最接近X和Y之间的相关性？（附加问题：如果我们翻转轴，那么相关性是多少？）
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item 0.28
    \item 0.41
    \item 0.64
    \item 1.50
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 因为$\rho(X,Y) = 1.50 \times \sqrt{3.813} / \sqrt{20.721} = 0.6435$
        \item 因为$\beta(Y,X) = \sigma(X,Y)/\sigma^2(X) = [\rho(X,Y)\sigma(X)\sigma(Y)]/\sigma^2(X) = \rho(X,Y)\sigma(Y)/\sigma(X)$，因此$\rho(X,Y) = \beta(Y,X)\sigma(X)/\sigma(Y)$; 在这种情况下，$\rho(X,Y) = 1.50 \times \sqrt{3.813}/\sqrt{20.721} = 0.643457$。注意，如果我们使用有偏方差，我们会得到相同的结果（！），因为$(n-1)$被抵消了：$\rho(X,Y) = 1.50 \times \sqrt{3.749}/\sqrt{20.376} = 0.643457$。
        \item 关于附加问题，相关性不会改变，尽管beta会改变。如果我们翻转轴，回归线是$y = 1.237 + 0.276x$，在这种情况下$\rho(X,Y) = 0.276 \times \sqrt{20.376}/\sqrt{3.749} = 0.643457$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{回归斜率（β）与相关系数的关系}{3}{2}

% --- Question 79 ---
\begin{question}
    The originate-to-distribute (OTD) banking model has both advantages and disadvantages. In regard to the OTD banking model, which of the following statements is TRUE?
    \\ originate-to-distribute（OTD）银行模式既有优势也有劣势。关于OTD银行模式，哪一项陈述是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item OTD depresses the originating bank's reported return on equity (ROE)
    \\OTD 降低了发起银行的报告股本回报率（ROE）
    \item OTD implies that investors should be aware of the adverse selection problem implied by information asymmetry
    \\OTD 意味着投资者应该意识到由信息不对称引起的逆向选择问题
    \item OTD has an inherent, unavoidable design bug and public relations problem: mezzanine and equity tranches absorb losses before senior tranches
