    \\OTD 有一个内在的、不可避免的设计缺陷和公关问题：夹层和股权分层在优先分层之前吸收损失
    \item OTD was banned after the global financial crisis (GFC 2007-08) such that it can no longer be conducted without incurring grave regulatory risk
    \\OTD 在2007-08年全球金融危机（GFC）之后被禁止，因此现在无法在没有严重监管风险的情况下进行
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于（A），OTD 缩小了资产负债表，并倾向于增加ROE
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item OTD 意味着投资者应该意识到由信息不对称引起的逆向选择问题
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于（C），从属是证券化（信用风险转移）的两个关键特征之一
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 关于（D），这大大高估了OTD在危机后的反应。如GARP解释：“ originate-to-distribute 模型在2007-2008危机中发挥了作用。银行放松了抵押贷款贷款标准，因此抵押贷款的质量下降。尽管如此，银行仍然能够将它们证券化。
        \item 事实上，他们通过从分层中创建分层来重新证券化抵押贷款。随着抵押贷款违约率上升，分层持有者遭受损失。不出所料，在危机之后的一段时间内， originate-to-distribute 模型无法使用，因为它不被投资者信任。”--- 来源：教育，Pearson。金融市场和产品。Pearson学习解决方案，2020。VitalBook 文件。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{发起-分销（OTD）模式与逆向选择}{3}{2}

    % --- Question 80 ---
\begin{question}
    Gigh Inc., a publicly traded company, had a wild swing today after OPEC cut production. Gigh ended the day with a -16\% loss. Prior to this (most recently), the daily volatility was estimated to be 4.40\% according to the exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) model. The EWMA model had originally assumed a lambda, $\lambda$, of 0.70, but David wants to adjust the model's responsiveness to recent market conditions. David notices that his error is too high, so he wants to change the model to respond more quickly to changes in volatility, so he decides to decrease $\lambda$ by 0.10. After David properly adjusts lambda, what is the new volatility estimate using EWMA?
    \\Gigh Inc.，一家上市公司，在OPEC减产后经历了一次大幅波动。Gigh 当天收盘时下跌了-16\%。在此之前（最近），根据指数加权移动平均（EWMA）模型，每日波动率估计为4.40\%。EWMA模型最初假设lambda，$\lambda$，为0.70，但David希望调整模型对近期市场条件的响应。David注意到他的误差过高，因此他希望改变模型，更快地响应波动率的变化，因此他决定将$\lambda$减少0.10。在David正确调整lambda后，使用EWMA计算的新波动率估计是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $\sigma = 8.16\%$
    \item $\sigma = 0.66\%$
    \item $\sigma = 10.67\%$
    \item $\sigma = 1.14\%$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $\sigma = 10.68\%$
        \item 较低的$\lambda$分配更多的权重给最近的观察，使得EWMA更快地响应波动率的变化。因此，为了使模型更快地响应波动率的上行，David应该减少$\lambda$。因此，
    \[ \sigma^2_n = \lambda * \sigma^2 + (1 - \lambda) * (\text{return})^2 \]
    \[ \text{New variance} = 0.6 * 4.40\%^2 + (1 - .6) * -16\%^2 = 0.0114016 \]
    \[ \text{Volatility} = \text{SQRT}(0.0114016) = 10.68\% \]
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{EWMA 波动率估计与衰减因子 λ}{3}{2}

% --- Question 81 ---
\begin{question}
    Assume the riskfree rate is $3.0\%$ while the market portfolio has a return of $13.0\%$ (i.e., excess return is $10.0\%$) with volatility of $15.0\%$. Further, the following four portfolios are observed:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Portfolio (A) has an expected return of $11.0\%$ with volatility of $8.0\%$
        \item Portfolio (B) has an expected return of $7.0\%$ with volatility of $20.0\%$ and its correlation to the market is $0.30$
        \item Portfolio (C) has a beta with respect to the market $\beta(C, M) = 1.50$ and its realized return of $+20.0\%$ implies an alpha of $+2.0\%$
        \item Portfolio (D) has a beta with respect to the market $\beta(C, M) = 0.40$ and its realized return of $+9.0\%$ implies an alpha of $+2.0\%$
    \end{itemize}
    Under the conditions of modern portfolio theory (MPT; mean-variance framework) and the capital asset pricing model (CAPM), each of the above portfolios is valid and plausible EXCEPT which is not possible?

    假设无风险利率为$3.0\%$，市场投资组合的收益率为$13.0\%$（即超额收益为$10.0\%$），波动率为$15.0\%$。此外，观察到以下四个投资组合：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合（A）的预期收益率为$11.0\%$，波动率为$8.0\%$
        \item 投资组合（B）的预期收益率为$7.0\%$，波动率为$20.0\%$，且与市场的相关系数为$0.30$
        \item 投资组合（C）相对于市场的贝塔系数$\beta(C, M) = 1.50$，其实现收益为$+20.0\%$，意味着阿尔法为$+2.0\%$
        \item 投资组合（D）相对于市场的贝塔系数$\beta(C, M) = 0.40$，其实现收益为$+9.0\%$，意味着阿尔法为$+2.0\%$
    \end{itemize}
    在现代投资组合理论（MPT，均值-方差框架）和资本资产定价模型（CAPM）的条件下，上述每个投资组合都是有效且合理的，除了哪一个是不可能的？
    
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Portfolio A
    \\投资组合A
    \item Portfolio B
    \\投资组合B
    \item Portfolio C
    \\投资组合C
    \item Portfolio D
    \\投资组合D
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合A是不现实的，因为它比市场投资组合“更有效”：它的夏普比率为$1.0$，而市场投资组合的夏普比率为$(13\% - 3\%)/15\% = 0.667$。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合B的贝塔系数$\beta(B, M) = 0.30 * 20.0\% / 15.0\% = 0.40$，CAPM预期回报为$E(B) = 3.0\% + 0.40 * 10.0\% = 7.0\%$
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合C的詹森阿尔法，$\alpha(J, C) = 20.0\% - 3.0\% - 1.50 * 10.0\% = +2.0\%$
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{D选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合D的詹森阿尔法，$\alpha(J, D) = 9.0\% - 3.0\% - 0.40 * 10.0\% = +2.0\%$
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{现代投资组合理论（MPT）与资本资产定价模型（CAPM）的适用性}{3}{2}

% --- Question 82 ---
\begin{question}
    In comparing and contrasting the law of large numbers (LLN) and the central limit theorem (CLT), each of the following statements is true EXCEPT which is false?
    \\在比较和对比大数定律（LLN）和中心极限定理（CLT）时，下列每个陈述都是正确的，除了哪一个是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item The LLN does not require independence but it does require normally distributed data
    \\大数定律不需要独立性，但需要正态分布的数据
    \item The CLT extends the LLN by requiring one additional assumption (the variance is finite)
    \\中心极限定理扩展了大数定律，需要一个额外的假设（方差是有限的）
    \item LLN says that as the sample size increases ($n \to \infty$) the $\text{Pr}(X_{\text{avg}} = \mu)$ approaches 100\%
    \\大数定律说，随着样本量增加（$n \to \infty$），$\text{Pr}(X_{\text{avg}} = \mu)$ 接近100\%
    \item CLT says that as the sample size increases the distribution of the sample mean converges on the normal distribution $N(\mu, \sigma^2/n)$
    \\中心极限定理说，随着样本量增加，样本均值的分布收敛于正态分布 $N(\mu, \sigma^2/n)$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    A
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A说法错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 实际情况相反：大数定律要求独立同分布(i.i.d.)的随机变量，但与中心极限定理类似，它对分布没有要求；大数定律当然不要求正态分布的随机变量。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{B说法恰当，不选。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 大数定律（LLN）指出，随着样本容量的增加（$n \to \infty$），$\text{Pr}(X_{\text{avg}} = \mu)$ 接近100\%。这就是大数定律的本质。正如 GARP 所说：“大数定律（LLN）确立了均值估计量在大样本下的性质，并给出了平均值收敛于期望的条件。对于独立同分布随机变量，最简单的大数定律就是 Kolmogorov 强大数定律。”——2020 FRM 一级定量分析，第10版。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C说法恰当，不选。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 中心极限定理（CLT）在大数定律的基础上增加了一个额外的假设：方差是有限的。大数定律只要求有限均值，而中心极限定理还要求方差有限。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{D说法恰当，不选。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item CLT 说明，随着样本容量的增加，样本均值的分布收敛于正态分布 $N(\mu, \sigma^2/n)$。这就是中心极限定理的精髓：它的神奇之处在于，无需了解随机变量的分布，只要样本是 i.i.d.、均值和方差有限，样本均值渐近服从正态分布。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{大数定律与中心极限定理的对比与适用性}{3}{2}

% --- Question 84 ---
\begin{question}
    Scott is a risk manager for an investment manager. He is interested in estimating the potential loss the portfolio can expect over a specific time frame at a given confidence level (VaR) and the average of all potential losses beyond that potential loss (aka, expected shortfall, ES). Over the past 120 days, the portfolio’s ten worst daily returns were the following:\\
    \begin{tabular}{|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|}
        \hline
        -6.90\% & -7.55\% & -7.43\% & -4.85\% & -15.69\% & -14.48\% & -0.97\% & -18.38\% & -16.54\% & -24.10\% \\
        \hline
    \end{tabular}
    
    Find the 1-day 5\% VaR and ES for Scott (per Ch 2 Calculating and Applying VaR).

    斯科特是一家投资管理公司的风险经理。他希望估算投资组合在特定时间范围和置信水平下可能遭受的最大损失（VaR），以及超过该最大损失的所有潜在损失的平均值（即，预期损失，ES）。在过去的120天里，该投资组合最差的10天日收益率如下：\\
    \begin{tabular}{|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|r|}
        \hline
        -6.90\% & -7.55\% & -7.43\% & -4.85\% & -15.69\% & -14.48\% & -0.97\% & -18.38\% & -16.54\% & -24.10\% \\
        \hline
    \end{tabular}
    
    请根据第一卷第二章“VaR的计算与应用”，为斯科特计算单日5\%的VaR和ES。
   
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item VaR 17.84\%, ES 7.55\%
    \item VaR 7.43\%, ES 16.12\%
    \item VaR 14.48\%, ES 16.12\%
    \item VaR 14.48\% ES 17.84\%
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 第一步：将值从最差到最差排列。
    \end{itemize}
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·84·1.png} 
    \end{figure}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 第二步：计算5\%百分位数 = $(5\% * 120) = 6\text{th worst day} + 1 = 7\text{th worst day}$ (per P2 Dowd).
        \item 但是，应该注意的是，其他章节将显示另一种计算VaR的方法，包括使用第6个最差、第7个最差或多次插值，包括使用第6个和第7个最差之间
        \item 第三步：识别第7个最差的日子，在我们的例子中是-7.43\%。这意味着有95\%的概率损失小于7.43\%。
        \item 计算ES：条件是损失超过VaR的平均值，而不是ES。然而，ES不是模糊的，总是5\%尾部的平均值，在这种情况下是6个最差损失。
        \end{itemize}
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·84·2.png} 
    \end{figure}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{历史模拟法下的 VaR 与预期损失（ES）}{3}{2}

% --- Question 85 ---
\begin{question}
    A portfolio with a volatility of 30.0\% has a Treynor measure of 0.080. The portfolio has a correlation of 0.50 with the market index which itself has a volatility of 20.0\%. What is the portfolio’s Sharpe measure?
    \\一个波动率为30.0\%的投资组合的Treynor测度为0.080。该投资组合与市场指数的相关系数为0.50，市场指数本身的波动率为20.0\%。该投资组合的Sharpe测度是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item 0.095
    \item 0.200
    \item 0.330
    \item 0.475
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $\text{beta}(P,M) = \text{correlation}(P,M) \times \text{volatility}(P) / \text{volatility}(M) = 0.50 \times 30\% / 20\% = 0.750$。
        \item 因为Treynor = (投资组合的超额回报)/beta，投资组合的超额回报 = $0.080 \times 0.750 = 6.0\%$ 和它的夏普比率 = $6.0\% / 30.0\% = 0.20$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{Sharpe 与 Treynor 测度及 β 的换算}{3}{2}

    以下是特征（即自变量）的文本标签：Amount, Term, Interest\_rate, Installment, Employ\_hist, Income, 以及 Bankruptcies。关于数据库中的单位，请注意：Amount 单位为千美元（$\dollar000$）；Term 单位为月；Interest\_rate 实际上乘以一百，因此7表示7\%或0.070；Installment 单位为美元；Employment\_hist 单位为年；Income 单位为千美元（$\dollar000$）；Bankruptcies 为整数$\{0, 1, 2, ...\}$。

    \item Each +100 basis points increase in the interest rate (e.g., from 8.0\% to 9.0\%), the expected default odds increase by about 15 percent

    \\这个回归的关键弱点是，Darlene无法覆盖基于标准$Z = 0.50$阈值的二元（是/否）分类，以避免接受太多申请

% --- Question 87 ---
\begin{question}
    Consider the following four mutual funds and their respective behaviors:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Fund A is an open-end fund that engages in ``late trading'' by accepting orders after $4$ pm
        \item Fund B engages in ``front running'' by allowing its traders to make profitable trades in their own accounts ahead of the fund's large block trades
        \item Fund C engages in ``directed brokerage'' because it has an informal, reciprocal arrangement that guarantees the fund will use the brokerage for trades but only if the brokerage recommends its clients to the mutual fund
        \item Fund D engages in ``timing the market'' because, among its watchlist of $100$ stocks, it buys (sells) whenever the price moves two standard deviations below (above) its $200$-day moving average
    \end{itemize}
    Each of these behaviors is undesirable (or illegal) EXCEPT which is acceptable; i.e., which of the following statements is TRUE?
    \\考虑以下四个共同基金及其各自的行为：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 基金A是一个开放式基金，通过在下午4点后接受订单进行“晚交易”
        \item 基金B通过允许其交易员在基金的大额交易之前在其自己的账户中进行盈利交易进行“抢先交易”
        \item 基金C通过“定向经纪”进行交易，因为它有一个非正式的、互惠的安排，保证基金只会使用经纪商进行交易，但只有在经纪商推荐其客户给共同基金时才会这样做
        \item 基金D通过“市场时机”进行交易，因为它在其100只股票的观察名单中，每当价格移动两个标准差低于（高于）其200天移动平均线时买入（卖出）
    \end{itemize}
    每个行为都是不受欢迎的（或非法的），除了哪一个是可接受的；即，以下哪个陈述是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Fund A's behavior is permitted by the SEC
    \\基金A的行为被SEC允许
    \item Fund B's behavior is legal
    \\基金B的行为是合法的
    \item Fund C's behavior is encouraged by regulators
    \\基金C的行为被监管机构鼓励
    \item Fund D's behavior is permitted by regulators
    \\基金D的行为被监管机构允许
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 基金D的行为（市场时机）被监管机构允许，并被许多投资者实践。这种时机与“市场时机”混淆，市场时机是指在下午4点购买/出售开放式共同基金份额，实际价值可能高于略微过时的净资产价值（NAV）。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{共同基金的行为类型与监管规定}{3}{2}

% --- Question 88 ---
\begin{question}
    Consider a six-month at-the-money (ATM) European call option on a non-dividend-paying stock with a current price of $\dollar80.00$. Peter the Risk Analyst has employed a two-step (i.e., three months per step) binomial model to price the option, as displayed below:
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·88.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Peter’s model matches the up and down movements to his estimate of the stock’s prospective volatility, which he assumes is $34.0\%$ per annum. The risk-free rate is $4.0\%$. Which of the following is nearest to the risk-neutral probability of the stock price going up in a single step?
    
    考虑一个到期期限为六个月的平值（ATM）欧式看涨期权，该期权标的是一只不支付股息的股票，当前价格为$\dollar80.00$。风险分析师Peter使用了两步二项树模型（即每步为三个月）对该期权定价，并如下面的图所示：
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·88.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Peter的模型将价格的上下变动与他对该股票预期波动率的估计相匹配，他假设年化波动率为$34.0\%$，无风险利率为$4.0\%$。下列哪个数值最接近股票价格在一步内向上变动的风险中性概率？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $45.61\%$
    \item $46.44\%$
    \item $48.70\%$
    \item $52.52\%$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $u = \exp[\sigma\sqrt{\Delta t}] = \exp[0.340\sqrt{0.250}] = 1.18530$,
        \item $d = \exp[-\sigma\sqrt{\Delta t}] \text{ 或 } 1/d = 0.84366$，和
        \item $p = [\exp(r\Delta t) - d]/(u-d) = [\exp(0.040 \times 0.25) - 0.84366]/(1.18530 - 0.84366) = 0.4870195$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{二项式期权定价中的风险中性概率（u、d 与 p）}{3}{2}

% --- Question 89 ---
\begin{question}
    About risk reporting practices, the Committee says “[51.] Accurate, complete and timely data is a foundation for effective risk management. However, data alone does not guarantee that the board and senior management will receive appropriate information to make effective decisions about risk. To manage risk effectively, the right information needs to be presented to the right people at the right time. Risk reports based on risk data should be accurate, clear and complete. They should contain the correct content and be presented to the appropriate decision-makers in a time that allows for an appropriate response. To effectively achieve their objectives, risk reports should comply with the following principles. Compliance with these principles should not be at the expense of each other ...”[1]

    Each of the following is a risk reporting principle EXCEPT which is not a risk reporting principle?

    [1] “Principles for Effective Data Aggregation and Risk Reporting,” (Basel Committee on Banking Supervision Publication, January 2013)

    关于风险报告实践，委员会表示：“[51.] 准确、完整和及时的数据是有效风险管理的基础。然而，仅有数据并不能保证董事会和高级管理层会收到足以做出有效风险决策的适当信息。为了有效管理风险，必须在正确的时间将正确的信息传递给正确的人。基于风险数据的报告应该准确、清晰且完整。它们应包含正确信息，并以允许及时响应的时间点呈现给适当的决策者。为了有效实现其目标，风险报告应遵循以下原则。这些原则的遵循不应以牺牲彼此为代价……”[1]

    下列各项都是风险报告原则，除了哪一项不是风险报告原则？

    [1] “有效数据聚合与风险报告原则”（巴塞尔银行监管委员会出版物，2013年1月）
    
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item Accuracy
        \\准确性
        \item Comprehensiveness
        \\全面性
        \item Clarity and usefulness
        \\清晰和有用性
        \item Manual workarounds
        \\手动工作
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    D
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 

    \begin{itemize}
        \item D. “手动工作（Manual workarounds）”是指“采用人工流程和工具来传输、处理或更改用于数据聚合或报告的数据”。在风险数据聚合的监管指引中（第39条），手动工作被专门提及：监管机构要求银行记录并解释所有风险数据聚合过程，无论是自动化还是人工（基于判断或其他方式）。文档应包括对任何手动工作的合理性说明、这些手动方法对风险数据聚合准确性的重要性描述，以及拟定的减缓影响的措施。[1]
        \item A、B、C三项都是风险报告的原则：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item 原则7：准确性（Accuracy）——风险管理报告应准确、精确地传达聚合风险数据，准确反映风险。报告应当核对和验证。
            \item 原则8：全面性（Comprehensiveness）——风险管理报告应涵盖组织内所有重大风险领域。报告深度和范围应与银行规模、复杂性、风险特征及接收者需求相一致。
            \item 原则9：清晰和有用性（Clarity and usefulness）——风险管理报告应清晰、简洁地传达信息。报告既易于理解，又要足够全面，以便支持知情决策。报告应平衡数据、分析和解释，并结合定性说明，针对不同接收者需要呈现有意义的信息。
            \item 原则10：频率（Frequency）——董事会和高管（或相关接收者）应设定风险报告的制作和分发表频率，结合接收者需求、风险性质、风险变化速度及报告在风险管理和决策中的重要性。在压力或危机时期，报告频率应适当增加。
            \item 原则11：分发（Distribution）——风险管理报告应分发给相关方，同时保证信息的保密性。[1]
        \end{itemize}

        [1] “有效数据聚合和风险报告原则,” (巴塞尔银行监管委员会出版物, 2013年1月)
    \end{itemize}
    \end{explanation}
    \checklist{巴塞尔有效风险数据聚合与风险报告原则}{3}{2}

% --- Question 90 ---
\begin{question}
    Peter wants to model the following AR(2) time series: $Y(t) = 0.750*Y(t-1) - 0.1250*T(t-2) + e(t)$. He wonders if this AR(2) is stationary. He realizes that he can write this as a lag polynomial:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $Y(t) = 0.750*L - 0.1250*L^2 + \varepsilon(t)$; first he expresses the model with lag operators
        \item $(1 - 0.750*L + 0.1250*L^2)*Y(t) = \varepsilon(t)$; then he isolates the lag polynomial
        \item $(1 - 0.50*L)(1 - 0.250*L)*Y(t) = \varepsilon(t)$; finally, he factors the polynomial
    \end{itemize}
    Which of the following statements about this AR(2) time series is true?

    彼得想对以下AR(2)时间序列进行建模：$Y(t) = 0.750*Y(t-1) - 0.1250*T(t-2) + e(t)$。他想知道这个AR(2)是否是平稳的。他意识到他可以将其写成滞后多项式：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $Y(t) = 0.750*L - 0.1250*L^2 + \varepsilon(t)$; 首先他用滞后算子表示模型
        \item $(1 - 0.750*L + 0.1250*L^2)*Y(t) = \varepsilon(t)$; 然后他用滞后多项式隔离
        \item $(1 - 0.50*L)(1 - 0.250*L)*Y(t) = \varepsilon(t)$; 最后他用多项式因式分解
    \end{itemize}
    下列关于这个AR(2)时间序列的陈述中哪一个是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item This AR(2) is non-stationary and Peter incorrectly factored the polynomial
    \\这个AR(2)是非平稳的，彼得错误地因式分解了多项式
    \item This AR(2) is non-stationary because the sum of the coefficients is less than one
    \\这个AR(2)是非平稳的，因为系数之和小于一
    \item This AR(2) is stationary because the sum of the coefficients is less than one
    \\这个AR(2)是平稳的，因为系数之和小于一
    \item This AR(2) is stationary because both roots of the characteristic equation are greater than one
    \\这个AR(2)是平稳的，因为特征方程的两个根都大于一
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 这个AR(2)是平稳的，因为特征方程的两个根都大于一
        \item 给定这个因式分解的多项式，$(1 - 0.50*L)(1 - 0.250*L)*Y(t)$，根求解：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item $(1 - 0.50*L) = 0$ 使得 $L = 2.0$；和
            \item $(1 - 0.250*L) = 0$ 使得 $L = 4.0$。
        \end{itemize}
        \item 如果两个根的绝对值都大于一（1.0），那么过程是平稳的。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{AR(2)时间序列的平稳性检验与特征根分析}{3}{2}

% --- Question 91 ---
\begin{question}
    The daily standard deviation of Bitcoin (BTC) is \dollar200.00 while the standard deviation of the futures contract price (on the same BTC) is \dollar340.00. The correlation between the two changes is 0.850. If the size of one futures contract is five Bitcoins (5 BTC), and the trader seeks to hedge the purchase of 100 Bitcoins (100 BTC), then how many contracts should be shorted to hedge?

    比特币（BTC）的每日标准差为$\dollar200.00$，而期货合约价格（与BTC相关）的标准差为$\dollar340.00$。这两个变化的关联性为0.850。如果一个期货合约的价值为五个比特币（5 BTC），而交易者希望对购买100比特币（100 BTC）进行对冲，那么应该做空多少个合约？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item One (1)
    \\一（1）
    \item Three (3)
    \\三（3）
    \item Ten (10)
    \\十（10）
    \item Twenty five (25)
    \\二十五（25）
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 最优对冲比率，$h^*$，由$h^* = \beta(\Delta S/\Delta F) = \rho \cdot \sigma(S)/\sigma(F)$给出；在这种情况下，$h^* = 0.850 \cdot \dollar200.00/\dollar340.00 = 0.50$。
        \item 做空的合约数量由$N^* = h^* \times Q_A/Q_F$给出；在这种情况下，$N^* = 0.50 \cdot 100/5 = 10.0$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{期货对冲策略的最优对冲比率与合约数量计算}{3}{2}

% --- Question 92 ---
\begin{question}
    After Barbara wrote (i.e., sold) 100 put option contracts, the trade's position delta was +7,200. The put options were in-the-money as the stock price was \dollar70.00 while the options' strike price was \dollar100.00. Each contract was for 100 put options. The percentage gamma of each put option was +0.0120. If the stock price subsequently, immediately decreased by \dollar10.00 to \dollar60.00, which is \textbf{nearest} to an estimate of the trade's new position delta?

    巴勃拉卖出（即出售）了100份看跌期权合约，该交易的头寸delta为+7,200。看跌期权处于价内状态，因为股票价格为$\dollar70.00$，而期权的行权价为$\dollar100.00$。每个合约包含100份看跌期权。每个看跌期权的百分比gamma为+0.0120。如果股票价格随后立即下跌$\dollar10.00$至$\dollar60.00$，哪一项最接近对该交易新头寸delta的估计？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item -3,500
    \item +7,200 (unchanged)
    \item +8,400
    \item +11,900
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 空头认沽期权头寸的百分比delta为$7,200/-10,000 = -0.720$。
        \item 已知每个认沽期权的百分比gamma为$+0.0120$，所以当股票价格下跌$\dollar10.00$时，delta的变化为$-\dollar10.00 \times 0.0120 = -0.120$，因此新的delta为$-0.720 - 0.120 = -0.840$。
        \item 注意，随着股票价格的下跌，认沽期权进一步深度价内，百分比delta会继续减少（趋向于-1.0）。因此，新头寸的delta为$-10,000 \times -0.840 = +8,400$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

    \checklist{期权 Delta 与 Gamma 对头寸损益的影响}{3}{2}

% --- Question 93 ---
\begin{question}
    The riskfree rate is $3.0\%$ and the market portfolio's expected return is $10.0\%$ (put another way, the market's excess expected return is $7.0\%$). Consider two portfolios:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Portfolio A has a high volatility, $\sigma(A) = 50.0\%$ per annum, but its correlation to the market portfolio is only, $\rho(A, M) = 0.30$
        \item Portfolio B has a moderate volatility, $\sigma(B) = 30.0\%$ per annum, and its correlation to the market portfolio is, $\rho(B, M) = 0.70$
    \end{itemize}
    If both portfolios lie on the security market line (SML), and if Portfolio A has an expected return, $ER(A) = 7.20\%$, then what is the expected return of Portfolio B?
    无风险利率为$3.0\%$，市场投资组合的预期收益率为$10.0\%$（换句话说，市场的超额预期收益为$7.0\%$）。考虑两个投资组合：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 投资组合A具有高波动性，$\sigma(A) = 50.0\%$ 每年，但其与市场投资组合的相关系数仅为$\rho(A, M) = 0.30$
        \item 投资组合B具有中等波动性，$\sigma(B) = 30.0\%$ 每年，其与市场投资组合的相关系数为$\rho(B, M) = 0.70$
    \end{itemize}
    如果两个投资组合都位于证券市场线（SML）上，且投资组合A的预期收益为$ER(A) = 7.20\%$，那么投资组合B的预期收益是多少？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item 5.100\%
    \item 7.900\%
    \item 8.880\%
    \item 9.540\%
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 如果两个投资组合都位于证券市场线（SML）上，那么$ER(A) = Rf + \beta(A,M) * [ER(M) - Rf]$ 和 $ER(B) = Rf + \beta(B,M) * [ER(M) - Rf]$。我们可以推断投资组合A的beta：因为$7.20\% = 3.0\% + \beta(A, M) * 7.0\%$，所以$\beta(A, M) = 0.60$。
        \item 而且因为$\beta(A, M) = \rho(A, M)*\sigma(A)/\sigma(M)$，我们可以推断$\sigma(M) = [\rho(A, M)*\sigma(A)]/\beta(A, M) = [0.30*0.50]/0.60 = 0.25$。
        \item 然后我们可以计算投资组合B的beta：$\beta(B, M) = \rho(B, M)*\sigma(B)/\sigma(M) = 0.70*0.30/0.25 = 0.840$。
        \item 投资组合B的预期收益由$ER(B) = 3.0\% + 0.840 * 7.0\% = 8.880\%$给出。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{证券市场线（SML）与 β 的推算（由相关系数与波动率）}{3}{3}

% --- Question 94 ---
\begin{question}
    Below are plotted the autocorrelation function (ACF) and partial autocorrelation function (PACF) for a simulated time-series process. Please note that the horizontal dashed blue lines represent the 95.0\% confidence interval.
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·94.png} 
    \end{figure}
    Which of the following time-series models is \textbf{most likely} described by these ACF and PACF plots?
    下图展示了模拟时间序列过程的自相关函数（ACF）和偏自相关函数（PACF）。请注意，水平虚蓝线代表95.0\%的置信区间。
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·94.png} 
    \end{figure}
    下列哪个时间序列模型最有可能被这些ACF和PACF图表描述？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item MA(1) with a coefficient of 1.3
    \\一阶移动平均模型，系数为1.3
    \item MA(3) with coefficients of 0.4, 0.6, and 0.8
    \\三阶移动平均模型，系数为0.4, 0.6, 和0.8
    \item AR(2) with coefficients of 1.5 and 0.8
    \\二阶自回归模型，系数为1.5和0.8
    \item AR(3) with coefficients of 0.3, -1.4 and 0.7
    \\三阶自回归模型，系数为0.3, -1.4和0.7
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：MA(3) with coefficients of 0.4, 0.6, and 0.8
        \item ACF在3个滞后后截断（即，在3个滞后后急剧下降）。PACF逐渐衰减。这表明一个MA(3)过程。
        \item GARP解释：“ACF对于大于$q$的滞后总是为零，PACF也是复杂的。一般来说，MA($q$)的PACF在所有滞后处都不为零... PACF振荡并缓慢趋向于零... 这两个[图10.6]显示了相同的模式---一个关键的区分MA模型和AR模型的模式：ACF在MA的阶数处截断，而PACF振荡并缓慢趋向于零。这与图10.2和图10.4中的模式相反，其中ACF缓慢衰减，而PACF截断。”--- 2020 FRM Part I: Quantitative Analysis, 10th Edition.
    \end{itemize}
    
\end{explanation}

\checklist{时间序列模型的 ACF 与 PACF 特征分析}{3}{2}

% --- Question 95 ---
\begin{question}
    Peter is a new analyst who has been asked to perform a valuation of a mortgage-backed security (MBS) using one of the firm’s models. The pool is likely to be stripped into interest-only (IO) and principal-only (PO) strips. Each of the following statements is true EXCEPT which is false?

    彼得是一名新分析师，被要求使用公司的一个模型对抵押贷款支持证券（MBS）进行估值。该池可能被剥离为利息支付（IO）和本金支付（PO）。下列每个陈述都是正确的，除了哪一个是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Interest rate uncertainty tends to reduce the value of IOs but increase the value of POs
    \\利率不确定性倾向于降低IO的价值，但增加PO的价值
    \item His prepayment model will include an annualized prepayment rate that is likely to predict refinancings according to an incentive function
    \\他的提前还款模型将包括一个年化提前还款率，该率可能根据激励函数预测再融资
    \item The prepayment rate in his model can be affected (in addition to interest rate levels) by average loan size, average loan age, season (aka, seasonality), and geographical location
    \\他的提前还款率可以受到（除了利率水平之外）平均贷款规模、平均贷款年龄、季节（即季节性）和地理位置的影响
    \item His preferred model is a recombining tree because the cash flows are path independent because the permutation of up/down jumps prior to arrival at a future interest rate not is irrelevant
    \\他的首选模型是一个重构树，因为现金流是路径独立的，因为提前到达未来利率的上升/下降跳跃的排列是不相关的
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
D
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{D选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 实际上，他需要对路径依赖性进行建模，因此会更倾向于使用蒙特卡洛模拟。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{抵押贷款支持证券（MBS）的现金流特征与提前还款模型}{3}{2}

% --- Question 96 ---
\begin{question}
    Trader Joe (who is unrelated to the awesome grocery store!) takes a long position in 100 out-of-the-money (OTM) put option contracts when the non-dividend-paying stock price is $\dollar88.00$ and its volatility is $28.0\%$; each contract is for 100 options. The strike price is $\dollar80.00$, and the option term is six months. The risk-free rate is $5.0\%$. The value of each option is $\dollar4.38$, and the position's value is $\dollar43,800.00$. With respect to these puts, the immediate percentage delta, $\Delta = -0.2550$ and gamma, $\Gamma = 0.0130$. If the stock price jumps by $+\dollar7.00$ to $\dollar95.00$, which is nearest to the position's value as approximated by delta and gamma; i.e., without a full re-pricing of the position?

    交易员乔（与著名的杂货店无关！）在非分红股票价格为$\dollar88.00$且波动率为$28.0\%$时买入100份价外（OTM）看跌期权合约；每个合约包含100份期权。行权价为$\dollar80.00$，期权期限为六个月。无风险利率为$5.0\%$。每个期权的价值为$\dollar4.38$，该头寸的价值为$\dollar43,800.00$。关于这些看跌期权，立即的百分比delta为$\Delta = -0.2550$，gamma为$\Gamma = 0.0130$。如果股票价格跳涨$\dollar7.00$至$\dollar95.00$，哪一项最接近该头寸的价值，即基于delta和gamma的近似值，不进行完整的价格重新计算？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item $\dollar18,280$
    \item $\dollar25,950$
    \item $\dollar29,135$
    \item $\dollar33,640$
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item delta-gamma近似变化由$\Delta S \cdot \Delta + 0.5 \cdot \Gamma \cdot (\Delta S)^2$给出；在这种情况下，$\Delta S = + 7.00$，估计变化$= 7.0 \cdot (-0.2550) + 0.5 \cdot (0.0130) \cdot 7.0^2 = -1.4665$。每个期权估计价值为$\dollar4.38 - \dollar1.4665 = \dollar2.9135$，头寸估计价值为$\dollar29,135.00$。
        \item 关于错误的（B），$\dollar25,950$是基于只有delta近似估计的价值；即，如果排除gamma调整项。
        \item 精确（重新定价为BSM）价值为$\dollar28,841$，所以近似值相差约$1.0\%$。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{期权 Delta-Gamma 近似与头寸价值变动}{3}{2}


% --- Question 97 ---
\begin{question}
    Which of the following is TRUE about the bank's audit function?

    关于银行审计功能，下列哪个陈述是正确的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Internal audit is necessary because it is virtually impossible to rate (i.e., assign ratings to) the risk management function
    \\内部审计是必要的，因为几乎不可能对风险管理功能进行评级（即分配评级）
    \item The firm's operational risk management should report directly to the internal audit function (who will, therefore, oversee risk management)
    \\公司的运营风险管理应直接向内部审计功能报告（因此将监督风险管理）
    \item Internal auditors are responsible for reviewing monitoring procedures, tracking the progress of risk management system upgrades, and affirming the efficacy of vetting processes
    \\内部审计师负责审查监控程序，跟踪风险管理系统的升级进度，并确认筛选过程的有效性
    \item The assistance of internal audit should not be required to the risk governance function: a properly designed risk governance function should be able to ascertain compliance alone
    \\风险治理功能不应需要内部审计的协助：一个合理设计的风险治理功能应能够独立确认合规性
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    C
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    
    \textbf{A选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item GARP建议评级风险管理实践：“风险管理功能可以被评级。这种评级可以由内部或第三方（例如评级机构）使用，用于对多家企业的比较分析。没有一种公式可以完美地管理风险。尽管如此，风险管理实践的评级对于促进组织内的比较是有益的，这样内部和外部各方都可以从中受益于客观的批评。”，GARP解释（2020 FRM Part I: Foundations of Risk Management, 10th Edition. Pearson Learning Solutions）
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{B选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 风险管理必须独立于内部审计：“在行业内，关于审计功能是否应该对公司的运营风险管理进行有效监督，一直存在着活跃的辩论。请注意，审计对内部控制的质量有天然的兴趣。然而，尽管受到审计师的审查，风险管理的实施必须与审计职能保持分离。作为一个基本原则，审计师对底层活动的独立性是确保审计师对董事会提供的任何保证或意见的信心所必需的，这也适用于风险管理功能及其相关的流程。除非这种独立性得到维护，否则利益冲突可能会损害风险管理和审计活动的质量，并严重危及风险治理。”，GARP解释。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{C选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 内部审计师负责审查监控程序，跟踪风险管理系统的升级进度，并确认筛选过程的有效性。
    \end{itemize}

    \textbf{D选项错误。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 内部审计是风险管理的重要补充：“遵守[风险管理]过程可以防止过度风险的假设。然而，风险治理功能本身无法确认遵守董事会制定的政策和外部法规。这就是审计功能的作用。内部审计职能有责任确保风险管理/治理的设置、实施和有效性。”，GARP解释。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{银行审计功能}{1}{1}

% --- Question 98 ---
\begin{question}
    For each of three banks (Azure Bank, Breeze Bank, and Coastal Bank), Stella collected the following data on three features:
    
    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·98.png} 
    \end{figure}
    
    Statistics displayed (top-right panel) include the mean, sample standard deviation (STDEV.S), and population standard deviation (STDEV.P). The standardized features (lower-left panel) are based on the sample standard deviation.
    
    With respect to the standardized features (not the raw values), which of the following is \textbf{nearest} to the Euclidean distance between Breeze Bank and Coastal Bank?

    对于三家银行（Azure Bank、Breeze Bank 和 Coastal Bank），Stella 收集了以下三个特征的数据：

    \begin{figure}[H]
        \centering
        \includegraphics[width=0.6\textwidth]{1·98.png} 
    \end{figure}

    显示的数据（右上角面板）包括均值、样本标准差（STDEV.S）和总体标准差（STDEV.P）。标准化特征（左下角面板）是基于样本标准差计算的。

    针对标准化特征（而非原始值），下列哪个数值最接近 Breeze Bank 与 Coastal Bank 之间的欧几里得距离？
\end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item 2.40
        \item 4.15
        \item 93.63
        \item 119.50
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    A
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}
    \textbf{A选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 正确的：2.40是Breeze Bank和Coastal Bank标准化特征之间的欧几里得距离。
        \item Breeze和Coastal之间的欧几里得距离由：
        $$ \sqrt{(-0.2930 - 1.1140)^2 + (-0.3470 - 1.1270)^2 + (-0.1880 - 1.0810)^2} = 2.40056 $$
        \item 关于（B）、（C）和（D），每项都是错误的。相反，它们是Breeze Bank和Coastal Bank之间的替代测量。具体来说：
        \begin{itemize}
            \item 4.15是它们标准化特征之间的曼哈顿距离
            \item 93.63是它们原始特征之间的欧几里得距离
            \item 119.50是它们原始特征之间的曼哈顿距离
        \end{itemize}
    \end{itemize}
    \end{explanation}
    
    \checklist{标准化特征下的距离度量（欧氏与曼哈顿）}{3}{2}


% --- Question 99 ---
\begin{question}
    Among over-the-counter (OTC) interest rate derivatives, interest rate swap contracts are the most actively traded, according to BIS (source: https://www.bis.org/publ/qtrpdf/r\_qt2212e.htm). Further, as Libor phases out, swaps will increasingly reference overnight rates such as SOFR and SONIA. About these modern overnight indexed swap (OIS) contracts, each of the following statements is true EXCEPT which is false?

    根据BIS（来源：https://www.bis.org/publ/qtrpdf/r\_qt2212e.htm），场外（OTC）利率衍生品中，利率互换合约是最活跃交易的，而随着Libor的逐步退出，互换将越来越多地参考隔夜利率，如SOFR和SONIA。关于这些现代隔夜指数互换（OIS）合约，下列每个陈述都是正确的，除了哪一个是错误的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item In contrast to LIBOR, overnight rates such as SOFR and SONIA are nearly (or effectively) risk-free rates
    \\与LIBOR相比，隔夜利率（如SOFR和SONIA）几乎是（或实际上是无风险的）无风险利率
    \item The $N$-year swap rate is set equal to the average yield to maturity of bonds trading with $N$-year maturities
    \\$N$年互换利率等于$N$年到期债券的平均到期收益率
    \item While LIBOR rates are known at the beginning of a period, overnight rates are known only at the end of each period
    \\虽然LIBOR利率在期初是已知的，但隔夜利率只有在每个期末是已知的
    \item The confirmation specifies the swap's day count convention, payment exchange dates, payment calculation methodology, and the applicable holiday calendar
    \\确认规定了互换的日计数惯例、支付交换日期、支付计算方法以及适用的节假日日历
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item $N$年互换利率是市场$N$年面值收益率，因为它是$N$年票面利率，目前初始互换以面值定价。换句话说，互换利率是面值收益率。另一方面，当前$N$年债券的到期收益率（aka，收益率）会随着它们的票面利率而变化。
        \item 关于（A）、（C）和（D），每项都是正确的。
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{利率互换合约的交易活跃度与现代OIS合约的特点}{3}{2}

% --- Question 100 ---
\begin{question}
    Analyst Patricia is analyzing the following four bonds:
    \begin{itemize}
        \item Bond A is a $\dollar100.00$ face value bond with $7.0$ years to maturity that pays a monthly coupon at a rate of $6.0\%$ per annum and offers a yield of $5.0\%$ per annum (with monthly compound frequency)
        \item Bond B is a $\dollar100.00$ face value bond with $10.0$ years to maturity that pays a semi-annual coupon at a rate of $4.0\%$ per annum and offers a yield of $5.0\%$ per annum (with semi-annual compound frequency)
        \item Bond C is a $\dollar100.00$ face value bond with $10.0$ years to maturity that pays an annual coupon at a rate of $7.0\%$ per annum and offers a yield of $6.0\%$ per annum (with annual compound frequency)
        \item Bond D is a $\dollar1,000.00$ face value zero-coupon bond with $30.0$ years to maturity that offers a yield (aka, yield to maturity) of $8.0\%$ per annum with semi-annual compound frequency
    \end{itemize}
    In terms of their current theoretical prices, which bonds are, respectively, the cheapest and most expensive (among the four)?

    分析师帕特丽夏正在分析以下四种债券：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 债券A是一张面值为$\dollar100.00$、期限为7年的债券，每月支付6\%的年利率，年化收益率为5\%（每月复利频率）
        \item 债券B是一张面值为$\dollar100.00$、期限为10年的债券，每半年支付4\%的年利率，年化收益率为5\%（半年复利频率）
        \item 债券C是一张面值为$\dollar100.00$、期限为10年的债券，每年支付7\%的年利率，年化收益率为6\%（年复利频率）
        \item 债券D是一张面值为$\dollar1,000.00$、期限为30年的零息债券，年化收益率为8\%（半年复利频率）
    \end{itemize}
    按照当前的理论价格，哪两种债券分别是四种债券中最便宜和最昂贵的？
\end{question}

\begin{choices}
    \item Bond A is the cheapest and Bond D is the most expensive
    \\债券A是最便宜的，债券D是最昂贵的
    \item Bond B is the cheapest and Bond C is the most expensive
    \\债券B是最便宜的，债券C是最昂贵的
    \item Bond C is the cheapest and Bond A is the most expensive
    \\债券C是最便宜的，债券A是最昂贵的
    \item Bond D is the cheapest and Bond B is the most expensive
    \\债券D是最便宜的，债券B是最昂贵的
\end{choices}

\begin{correctanswer}
    B
\end{correctanswer}

\begin{explanation}
    \textbf{B选项正确。} 
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 债券B是最便宜的（在$\dollar92.21$），债券C是最昂贵的（在$\dollar107.36$）。
        \item 以下是理论价格：
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 债券A: $7*12 = 84$ N, $5/12 = 0.4167$ I/Y, $0.06*100/12 = 0.50$ PMT, $100$ FV and CPT PV [+/-] = $\dollar105.90$
        \item 债券B: $10*2 = 20$ N, $5/2 = 2.5$ I/Y, $0.04*100/2 = 2.0$ PMT, $100$ FV and CPT PV [+/-] = $\dollar92.21$
        \item 债券C: $10*1 = 10$ N, $6/1 = 6.0$ I/Y, $0.07*100/1 = 7.0$ PMT, $100$ FV and CPT PV [+/-] = $\dollar107.36$
        \item 债券D: $30*2 = 60$ N, $8/2 = 4.0$ I/Y, $0$ PMT, $100$ FV and CPT PV [+/-] = $\dollar95.06$
    \end{itemize}
    \end{itemize}
\end{explanation}

\checklist{债券理论价格与不同付息/复利频率}{3}{2}








































 \begin{question}
    \color{red}Alex is a junior analyst in the operational risk department at Global Trust Bank. His manager Sarah asks him to classify recent operational risk events according to Basel Committee's seven event categories. Which operational risk is correctly categorized?\\
    Alex是Global Trust Bank运营风险部门的一名初级分析师。他的经理Sarah要求他根据巴塞尔委员会的七个事件类别对最近的运营风险事件进行分类。哪一种运营风险被正确分类？
    \end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item Reuters reports that a senior trader at Global Trust Bank manipulated the LIBOR submission process for three years, resulting in \dollar5 million illicit gains. Alex categorizes this as business disruption and system failures.\\
        Reuters报道，Global Trust Bank的一名高级交易员连续三年操纵LIBOR提交过程，非法获利500万美元。Alex将此事归类为业务中断和系统故障。
        
        \item The bank's core trading platform experienced a four-hour outage during peak market hours, causing significant trading delays. Alex classifies this as execution, delivery, and process management.\\
        银行的核心交易平台在高峰市场时段发生四小时宕机，导致重大交易延误。Alex将此事归类为执行、交付和流程管理。
        
        \item A severe earthquake damaged several branch offices in the coastal region, leading to \dollar20 million in property losses. Alex categorizes this as internal fraud.\\
        一场严重的地震毁坏了沿海地区的一些分支机构，造成2000万美元的财产损失。Alex将此事归类为内部欺诈。
        
        \item Local media reveals that external hackers breached the bank's security system and stole sensitive client data, demanding \dollar3 million in ransom. Alex classifies this as external fraud.\\
        当地媒体透露，外部黑客入侵了银行的安保系统，窃取了敏感的客户数据，并索要300万美元的赎金。Alex将此事归类为外部欺诈。
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    D
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}

    
    \textbf{A选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item LIBOR操纵属于内部欺诈范畴，因为涉及员工故意违规获取个人利益。该事件包含了欺诈、故意违反规定等典型的内部欺诈特征，而不是业务中断和系统故障。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 交易平台故障应归类为业务中断和系统故障，因为这涉及技术系统的运行中断。而执行、交付和流程管理通常指操作流程和交易处理中的人为错误。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 地震造成的财产损失应归类为实物资产损坏，这类风险源于自然灾害或其他外部事件对银行实物资产的破坏，而不是内部欺诈行为。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{D选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 外部黑客入侵和勒索属于典型的外部欺诈案例。这类事件由外部人员实施，目的是通过非法手段获取经济利益，符合外部欺诈的定义特征。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \end{explanation}
    
 \checklist{偿付能力II对保险公司的资本要求}{3}{3}




 \begin{question}

    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 地震造成的财产损失应归类为实物资产损坏，这类风险源于自然灾害或其他外部事件对银行实物资产的破坏，而不是内部欺诈行为。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{D选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 外部黑客入侵和勒索属于典型的外部欺诈案例。这类事件由外部人员实施，目的是通过非法手段获取经济利益，符合外部欺诈的定义特征。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \end{explanation}
    
 \checklist{偿付能力II对保险公司的资本要求}{3}{3}




 \begin{question}
    Alex is a junior analyst in the operational risk department at Global Trust Bank. His manager Sarah asks him to classify recent operational risk events according to Basel Committee's seven event categories. Which operational risk is correctly categorized?\\
    Alex是Global Trust Bank运营风险部门的一名初级分析师。他的经理Sarah要求他根据巴塞尔委员会的七个事件类别对最近的运营风险事件进行分类。哪一种运营风险被正确分类？
    \end{question}
    
    \begin{choices}
        \item Reuters reports that a senior trader at Global Trust Bank manipulated the LIBOR submission process for three years, resulting in \dollar5 million illicit gains. Alex categorizes this as business disruption and system failures.\\
        Reuters报道，Global Trust Bank的一名高级交易员连续三年操纵LIBOR提交过程，非法获利500万美元。Alex将此事归类为业务中断和系统故障。
        
        \item The bank's core trading platform experienced a four-hour outage during peak market hours, causing significant trading delays. Alex classifies this as execution, delivery, and process management.\\
        银行的核心交易平台在高峰市场时段发生四小时宕机，导致重大交易延误。Alex将此事归类为执行、交付和流程管理。
        
        \item A severe earthquake damaged several branch offices in the coastal region, leading to \dollar20 million in property losses. Alex categorizes this as internal fraud.\\
        一场严重的地震毁坏了沿海地区的一些分支机构，造成2000万美元的财产损失。Alex将此事归类为内部欺诈。
        
        \item Local media reveals that external hackers breached the bank's security system and stole sensitive client data, demanding \dollar3 million in ransom. Alex classifies this as external fraud.\\
        当地媒体透露，外部黑客入侵了银行的安保系统，窃取了敏感的客户数据，并索要300万美元的赎金。Alex将此事归类为外部欺诈。
    \end{choices}
    
    \begin{correctanswer}
    D
    \end{correctanswer}
    
    \begin{explanation}

    
    \textbf{A选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item LIBOR操纵属于内部欺诈范畴，因为涉及员工故意违规获取个人利益。该事件包含了欺诈、故意违反规定等典型的内部欺诈特征，而不是业务中断和系统故障。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{B选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 交易平台故障应归类为业务中断和系统故障，因为这涉及技术系统的运行中断。而执行、交付和流程管理通常指操作流程和交易处理中的人为错误。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{C选项错误。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 地震造成的财产损失应归类为实物资产损坏，这类风险源于自然灾害或其他外部事件对银行实物资产的破坏，而不是内部欺诈行为。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \textbf{D选项正确。}
    \begin{itemize}
        \item 外部黑客入侵和勒索属于典型的外部欺诈案例。这类事件由外部人员实施，目的是通过非法手段获取经济利益，符合外部欺诈的定义特征。
    \end{itemize}
    
    \end{explanation}
    
 \checklist{偿付能力II对保险公司的资本要求}{3}{3}
